router.get("/payments/cancel", (_req, res) => {
  res.setHeader("Content-Type", "text/html");
  res.send(`<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><title>Отменено</title><style>body{margin:0;min-height:100vh;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:#0D1B2A;font-family:system-ui,sans-serif;color:#E8EDF2}.card{text-align:center;padding:40px;max-width:380px}.icon{font-size:64px;margin-bottom:16px}.title{font-size:24px;font-weight:700;color:#6B7E93;margin:0 0 12px}.sub{font-size:15px;opacity:.7;margin:0 0 24px}</style></head><body><div class="card"><div class="icon">❌</div><h1 class="title">Платёж отменён</h1><p class="sub">Вернись в приложение и попробуй снова.</p></div></body></html>`);
});

router.post("/payments/webhook", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  if (!STRIPE_SECRET_KEY || !STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET) {
    res.status(503).json({ error: "Webhook не настроен" });
    return;
  }

  const sig = req.headers["stripe-signature"];
  if (!sig) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Missing stripe-signature header" });
    return;
  }

  const Stripe = (await import("stripe")).default;
  const stripe = new Stripe(STRIPE_SECRET_KEY);

  let event: ReturnType<typeof stripe.webhooks.constructEvent>;
  try {
    event = stripe.webhooks.constructEvent(
      req.body as string | Buffer,
      sig,
      STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET
    );
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err }, "Stripe webhook signature verification failed");
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Webhook signature invalid" });
    return;
  }

  if (event.type === "checkout.session.completed") {
    const session = event.data.object as {
      metadata?: { citizenId?: string; minutes?: string };
      id: string;
    };
    const citizenId = parseInt(session.metadata?.citizenId ?? "", 10);
    const minutes = parseInt(session.metadata?.minutes ?? "", 10);

    if (!isNaN(citizenId) && !isNaN(minutes) && minutes > 0) {
      const [citizen] = await db
        .select()
        .from(citizensTable)
        .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
        .limit(1);

      if (citizen) {
        await db
          .update(citizensTable)
          .set({ balanceMinutes: citizen.balanceMinutes + minutes })
          .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId));

        logger.info(
          { citizenId, minutes, stripeSessionId: session.id },
          "Minutes credited after Stripe payment"
        );
      }
    }
  }

  res.json({ received: true });
});

export default router;

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/wallet.ts ---
import { Router, type IRouter } from "express";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { citizensTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { ethers } from "ethers";
import { z } from "zod";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";
import crypto from "node:crypto";

const router: IRouter = Router();

const NONCE_TTL_MINUTES = 10;

function buildSignMessage(citizenId: number, walletAddress: string, nonce: string): string {
  return [
    "Welcome to TimeNation — the Digital State where Time is Currency.",
    "",
    `Linking wallet to Passport ID: ${citizenId}`,
    `Wallet: ${walletAddress}`,
    `Nonce: ${nonce}`,
    `Expires in: ${NONCE_TTL_MINUTES} minutes`,
  ].join("\n");
}

router.get("/auth/wallet-nonce", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const parsed = z.object({
    walletAddress: z.string().min(10),
    citizenId: z.coerce.number().int().positive(),
  }).safeParse(req.query);

  if (!parsed.success) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "walletAddress and citizenId are required" });
    return;
  }

  const { walletAddress, citizenId } = parsed.data;

  const citizen = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (citizen.length === 0) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Citizen not found" });
    return;
  }

  if (citizen[0]!.walletVerified && citizen[0]!.walletAddress) {
    res.status(409).json({ error: "Wallet already verified for this citizen" });
    return;
  }

  const conflicting = await db
    .select({ id: citizensTable.id })
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.walletAddress, walletAddress.toLowerCase()))
    .limit(1);

  if (conflicting.length > 0 && conflicting[0]!.id !== citizenId) {
    res.status(409).json({ error: "This wallet address is linked to another citizen" });
    return;
  }

  const nonce = crypto.randomBytes(16).toString("hex");

  await db
    .update(citizensTable)
    .set({ walletAddress: walletAddress.toLowerCase(), walletNonce: nonce })
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId));

  const message = buildSignMessage(citizenId, walletAddress, nonce);

  req.log.info({ citizenId, walletAddress }, "Wallet nonce generated");

  res.json({ nonce, message });
});

const VerifyBody = z.object({
  citizenId: z.number().int().positive(),
  walletAddress: z.string().min(10),
  signature: z.string().min(10),
});

router.post("/auth/wallet-verify", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const parsed = VerifyBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message });
    return;
  }

  const { citizenId, walletAddress, signature } = parsed.data;

  const [citizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Citizen not found" });
    return;
  }

  if (!citizen.walletNonce) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "No nonce found. Request a nonce first." });
    return;
  }

  if (citizen.walletAddress !== walletAddress.toLowerCase()) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Wallet address does not match nonce session" });
    return;
  }

  const message = buildSignMessage(citizenId, walletAddress, citizen.walletNonce);

  let recoveredAddress: string;
  try {
    recoveredAddress = ethers.verifyMessage(message, signature);
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err }, "Invalid signature format");
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid signature format" });
    return;
  }

  if (recoveredAddress.toLowerCase() !== walletAddress.toLowerCase()) {
    res.status(401).json({
      error: "Signature verification failed — recovered address does not match",
      recovered: recoveredAddress.toLowerCase(),
      provided: walletAddress.toLowerCase(),
    });
    return;
  }

  await db
    .update(citizensTable)
    .set({ walletNonce: null, walletVerified: 1, walletAddress: walletAddress.toLowerCase() })
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId));

  const [updated] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  req.log.info({ citizenId, walletAddress }, "Wallet verified and linked");

  res.json({
    success: true,
    walletAddress: walletAddress.toLowerCase(),
    citizen: {
      ...updated,
      transferableBalance: (updated?.balanceMinutes ?? 0) - 1,
      lastActivity: updated?.lastActivity?.toISOString() ?? null,
      createdAt: updated!.createdAt.toISOString(),
    },
  });
});

router.delete("/auth/wallet-unlink", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const parsed = z.object({ citizenId: z.coerce.number().int().positive() }).safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "citizenId is required" });
    return;
  }

  await db
    .update(citizensTable)
    .set({ walletAddress: null, walletNonce: null, walletVerified: 0 })
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, parsed.data.citizenId));

  res.json({ success: true });
});

export default router;

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/timeline.ts ---
import { Router, type IRouter } from "express";
import { eq, and, gte, lte } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { citizensTable, timelineAnchorsTable, timelineCapsulesTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { z } from "zod";
import { logTimelineAnchorToSheets } from "../lib/googleSheets";
import { sendTransactionNotification } from "../services/telegramService";

const router: IRouter = Router();

const AnchorBody = z.object({
  citizenId: z.number().int().positive(),
  timelineCoord: z
    .string()
    .regex(
      /^\d{12}$/,
      "timelineCoord must be 12 digits: YYYYMMDDHHmm"
    ),
});

function validateCoord(coord: string): string | null {
  const year = parseInt(coord.slice(0, 4), 10);
  const month = parseInt(coord.slice(4, 6), 10);
  const day = parseInt(coord.slice(6, 8), 10);
  const hour = parseInt(coord.slice(8, 10), 10);
  const minute = parseInt(coord.slice(10, 12), 10);

  if (year < 1 || year > 9999) return "Год должен быть от 1 до 9999";
  if (month < 1 || month > 12) return "Месяц должен быть от 01 до 12";
  if (day < 1 || day > 31) return "День должен быть от 01 до 31";
  if (hour < 0 || hour > 23) return "Час должен быть от 00 до 23";
  if (minute < 0 || minute > 59) return "Минута должна быть от 00 до 59";
  return null;
}

function formatCoordHuman(coord: string): string {
  const year = coord.slice(0, 4).replace(/^0+/, "") || "1";
  const month = coord.slice(4, 6);
  const day = coord.slice(6, 8);
  const hour = coord.slice(8, 10);
  const min = coord.slice(10, 12);
  return `${day}.${month}.${year} ${hour}:${min}`;
}

router.post("/timeline/anchor", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const parsed = AnchorBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message });
    return;
  }

  const { citizenId, timelineCoord } = parsed.data;

  const validErr = validateCoord(timelineCoord);
  if (validErr) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: validErr });
    return;
  }

  const [citizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Citizen not found" });
    return;
  }

  const transferableBalance = citizen.balanceMinutes - 1;
  if (transferableBalance <= 0) {
    res.status(400).json({
      error: "Недостаточно минут. Родовая Минута не может быть использована для закрепления координаты.",
    });
    return;
  }

  const [existing] = await db
    .select()
    .from(timelineAnchorsTable)
    .where(eq(timelineAnchorsTable.timelineCoord, timelineCoord))
    .limit(1);

  if (existing) {
    if (existing.citizenId === citizenId) {
      res.status(409).json({ error: "Эта координата уже закреплена за вами" });
    } else {
      res.status(409).json({ error: "Эта координата уже занята другим гражданином" });
    }
    return;
  }

  const [anchor] = await db
    .insert(timelineAnchorsTable)
    .values({ timelineCoord, citizenId, status: "active" })
    .returning();

  const [updatedCitizen] = await db
    .update(citizensTable)
    .set({ balanceMinutes: citizen.balanceMinutes - 1 })
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .returning();

  req.log.info({ citizenId, timelineCoord }, "Timeline anchor created");

  const sheetsLogged = await logTimelineAnchorToSheets({
    timelineCoord,
    citizenId,
    citizenName: citizen.name,
    createdAt: anchor.createdAt.toISOString(),
  });

  const humanCoord = formatCoordHuman(timelineCoord);
  const tgMsg =
    `🕰 *TimeNation — Таймлайн*\n\n` +
    `⏳ Координата *${humanCoord}* успешно зафиксирована за вашим родом навсегда!\n\n` +
    `💰 Остаток баланса: *${updatedCitizen.balanceMinutes} мин*`;

  await sendTransactionNotification(citizenId, tgMsg).catch(() => {});

  res.status(201).json({
    anchor: { ...anchor, createdAt: anchor.createdAt.toISOString() },
    newBalance: updatedCitizen.balanceMinutes,
    sheetsLogged,
  });
});

router.get("/timeline/occupied", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const rawYear = req.query["year"];
  const rawMonth = req.query["month"];
  const rawDay = req.query["day"];

  const year = rawYear ? String(rawYear).padStart(4, "0") : null;
  const month = rawMonth ? String(rawMonth).padStart(2, "0") : null;
  const day = rawDay ? String(rawDay).padStart(2, "0") : null;

  let coordMin: string;
  let coordMax: string;

  if (year && month && day) {
    coordMin = `${year}${month}${day}0000`;
    coordMax = `${year}${month}${day}2359`;
  } else if (year && month) {
    coordMin = `${year}${month}010000`;
    coordMax = `${year}${month}312359`;
  } else if (year) {
    coordMin = `${year}010100000`;
    coordMax = `${year}12312359`;
  } else {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "At least year is required" });
    return;
  }

  const anchors = await db
    .select()
    .from(timelineAnchorsTable)
    .where(
      and(
        gte(timelineAnchorsTable.timelineCoord, coordMin),
        lte(timelineAnchorsTable.timelineCoord, coordMax)
      )
    );

  res.json(
    anchors.map((a) => ({
      timelineCoord: a.timelineCoord,
      citizenId: a.citizenId,
      createdAt: a.createdAt.toISOString(),
    }))
  );
});

// ── GET /timeline/my-anchors?citizenId=X ─────────────────────────────────────
// Returns all timeline anchors owned by a citizen, sorted chronologically.
router.get("/timeline/my-anchors", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const citizenId = parseInt(String(req.query["citizenId"] ?? ""), 10);
  if (isNaN(citizenId) || citizenId <= 0) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "citizenId обязателен" });
    return;
  }

  const anchors = await db
    .select()
    .from(timelineAnchorsTable)
    .where(eq(timelineAnchorsTable.citizenId, citizenId))
    .orderBy(timelineAnchorsTable.timelineCoord);

  res.json(
    anchors.map((a) => ({
      timelineCoord: a.timelineCoord,
      status: a.status,
      createdAt: a.createdAt.toISOString(),
    }))
  );
});

// ── POST /timeline/capsule ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Saves a semantic capsule (free-text meaning) for a citizen's timeline anchor.
const CapsuleBody = z.object({
  citizenId: z.number().int().positive(),
  timelineCoord: z.string().regex(/^\d{12}$/, "timelineCoord must be 12 digits"),
  text: z.string().min(1).max(1000),
});

router.post("/timeline/capsule", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const parsed = CapsuleBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? "Неверные данные" });
    return;
  }
  const { citizenId, timelineCoord, text } = parsed.data;

  const citizen = await db
    .select({ id: citizensTable.id })
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1)
    .then((r) => r[0]);

  if (!citizen) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Гражданин не найден" });
    return;
  }

  const anchor = await db
    .select({ id: timelineAnchorsTable.id })
    .from(timelineAnchorsTable)
    .where(and(eq(timelineAnchorsTable.citizenId, citizenId), eq(timelineAnchorsTable.timelineCoord, timelineCoord)))
    .limit(1)
    .then((r) => r[0]);

  if (!anchor) {
    res.status(403).json({ error: "Эта координата вам не принадлежит" });
    return;
  }

  const [capsule] = await db
    .insert(timelineCapsulesTable)
    .values({ citizenId, timelineCoord, text })
    .returning();

  res.status(201).json({ id: capsule!.id, timelineCoord, createdAt: capsule!.createdAt.toISOString() });
});

export default router;

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/deals.ts ---
import { Router, type IRouter } from "express";
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { dealsTable, citizensTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { eq, or } from "drizzle-orm";
import { z } from "zod";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";

const router: IRouter = Router();

// ─── Validation schemas ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const CreateDealBody = z.object({
  senderId: z.number().int().positive(),
  receiverId: z.number().int().positive().optional(),
  amount: z.number().int().min(1).max(10000),
  description: z.string().min(5).max(1000),
});

const ProofBody = z.object({
  receiverId: z.number().int().positive(),
  proofText: z.string().max(2000).optional(),
  proofUrl: z.string().url().optional(),
});

const ActorBody = z.object({
  citizenId: z.number().int().positive(),
});

// ─── POST /api/deals ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Create a new deal. Immediately locks `amount` from sender's balance (escrow).
router.post("/deals", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const parsed = CreateDealBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message }); return; }

  const { senderId, receiverId, amount, description } = parsed.data;

  const [sender] = await db
    .select({ id: citizensTable.id, balance: citizensTable.balanceMinutes })
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, senderId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!sender) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Отправитель не найден" }); return; }
  if (sender.balance - 1 < amount) {
    // -1 because founding minute cannot be transferred
    res.status(402).json({ error: `Недостаточно Дарминов. Доступно: ${sender.balance - 1} Д` });
    return;
  }

  // Lock minutes in escrow (deduct from sender immediately)
  await db
    .update(citizensTable)
    .set({ balanceMinutes: sender.balance - amount })
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, senderId));

  const [deal] = await db
    .insert(dealsTable)
    .values({ senderId, receiverId: receiverId ?? null, amount, description, status: "open" })
    .returning();

  req.log.info({ dealId: deal.id, senderId, amount }, "TiMETR escrow deal created");
  res.status(201).json(deal);
});

// ─── GET /api/deals?citizenId=X ───────────────────────────────────────────────
router.get("/deals", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const citizenId = z.number().int().positive().safeParse(Number(req.query["citizenId"]));
  if (!citizenId.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: "citizenId обязателен" }); return; }

  const deals = await db
    .select()
    .from(dealsTable)
    .where(or(
      eq(dealsTable.senderId, citizenId.data),
      eq(dealsTable.receiverId, citizenId.data),
    ))
    .orderBy(dealsTable.createdAt);

  res.json({ deals });
});

// ─── GET /api/deals/open ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Open marketplace — deals with no receiver assigned yet
router.get("/deals/open", async (_req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const deals = await db
    .select({
      id: dealsTable.id,
      senderId: dealsTable.senderId,
      senderName: citizensTable.name,
      amount: dealsTable.amount,
      description: dealsTable.description,
      status: dealsTable.status,
      createdAt: dealsTable.createdAt,
    })
    .from(dealsTable)
    .leftJoin(citizensTable, eq(dealsTable.senderId, citizensTable.id))
    .where(eq(dealsTable.status, "open"))
    .orderBy(dealsTable.createdAt);

  res.json({ deals });
});

// ─── GET /api/deals/:id ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
router.get("/deals/:id", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params["id"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid id" }); return; }

  const [deal] = await db.select().from(dealsTable).where(eq(dealsTable.id, id)).limit(1);
  if (!deal) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Сделка не найдена" }); return; }
  res.json(deal);
});

// ─── POST /api/deals/:id/accept-task ─────────────────────────────────────────
// Receiver volunteers to take an open deal (assigns themselves).
router.post("/deals/:id/accept-task", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params["id"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid id" }); return; }

  const parsed = ActorBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: "citizenId обязателен" }); return; }

  const [deal] = await db.select().from(dealsTable).where(eq(dealsTable.id, id)).limit(1);
  if (!deal) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Сделка не найдена" }); return; }
  if (deal.status !== "open") { res.status(409).json({ error: `Сделка уже в статусе «${deal.status}»` }); return; }
  if (deal.senderId === parsed.data.citizenId) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Нельзя принять собственную сделку" }); return; }

  const [updated] = await db
    .update(dealsTable)
    .set({ receiverId: parsed.data.citizenId, status: "accepted", updatedAt: new Date() })
    .where(eq(dealsTable.id, id))
    .returning();

  req.log.info({ dealId: id, receiverId: parsed.data.citizenId }, "Deal accepted by receiver");
  res.json(updated);
});

// ─── POST /api/deals/:id/proof ────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Receiver submits Proof-of-Work (text + optional URL).
router.post("/deals/:id/proof", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params["id"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid id" }); return; }

  const parsed = ProofBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message }); return; }

  const [deal] = await db.select().from(dealsTable).where(eq(dealsTable.id, id)).limit(1);
  if (!deal) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Сделка не найдена" }); return; }
  if (deal.status !== "accepted") { res.status(409).json({ error: "Доказательство можно отправить только для принятой сделки" }); return; }
  if (deal.receiverId !== parsed.data.receiverId) { res.status(403).json({ error: "Только исполнитель может отправить доказательство" }); return; }
  if (!parsed.data.proofText && !parsed.data.proofUrl) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Нужно указать proof_text или proof_url" });
    return;
  }

  const [updated] = await db
    .update(dealsTable)
    .set({
      proofText: parsed.data.proofText ?? null,
      proofUrl: parsed.data.proofUrl ?? null,
      status: "proof_submitted",
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    })
    .where(eq(dealsTable.id, id))
    .returning();

  req.log.info({ dealId: id }, "Proof of work submitted");
  res.json(updated);
});

// ─── POST /api/deals/:id/complete ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// Sender approves proof → releases escrow to receiver.
router.post("/deals/:id/complete", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params["id"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid id" }); return; }

  const parsed = ActorBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: "citizenId обязателен" }); return; }

  const [deal] = await db.select().from(dealsTable).where(eq(dealsTable.id, id)).limit(1);
  if (!deal) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Сделка не найдена" }); return; }
  if (deal.status !== "proof_submitted") { res.status(409).json({ error: "Нельзя завершить — доказательство ещё не отправлено" }); return; }
  if (deal.senderId !== parsed.data.citizenId) { res.status(403).json({ error: "Только отправитель может подтвердить сделку" }); return; }
  if (!deal.receiverId) { res.status(409).json({ error: "Получатель не назначен" }); return; }

  // Release escrow: credit receiver
  const [receiver] = await db
    .select({ balance: citizensTable.balanceMinutes })
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, deal.receiverId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!receiver) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Получатель не найден" }); return; }

  await db
    .update(citizensTable)
    .set({ balanceMinutes: receiver.balance + deal.amount })
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, deal.receiverId));

  const [updated] = await db
    .update(dealsTable)
    .set({ status: "completed", updatedAt: new Date() })
    .where(eq(dealsTable.id, id))
    .returning();

  req.log.info({ dealId: id, receiverId: deal.receiverId, amount: deal.amount }, "Deal completed, escrow released");
  res.json(updated);
});

// ─── POST /api/deals/:id/decline ──────────────────────────────────────────────
// Sender declines proof → escrow stays locked, status = declined (dispute phase).
router.post("/deals/:id/decline", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params["id"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid id" }); return; }

  const parsed = ActorBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: "citizenId обязателен" }); return; }

  const [deal] = await db.select().from(dealsTable).where(eq(dealsTable.id, id)).limit(1);
  if (!deal) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Сделка не найдена" }); return; }
  if (deal.status !== "proof_submitted") { res.status(409).json({ error: "Нечего отклонять — доказательство не отправлено" }); return; }
  if (deal.senderId !== parsed.data.citizenId) { res.status(403).json({ error: "Только отправитель может отклонить сделку" }); return; }

  const [updated] = await db
    .update(dealsTable)
    .set({ status: "declined", updatedAt: new Date() })
    .where(eq(dealsTable.id, id))
    .returning();

  req.log.info({ dealId: id }, "Deal declined by sender — escrow remains locked");
  res.json({ ...updated, note: "Эскроу заморожен. Откройте спор или отмените сделку для возврата." });
});

// ─── POST /api/deals/:id/cancel ───────────────────────────────────────────────
// Sender cancels deal (open or declined) → returns escrow to sender.
router.post("/deals/:id/cancel", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params["id"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid id" }); return; }

  const parsed = ActorBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: "citizenId обязателен" }); return; }

  const [deal] = await db.select().from(dealsTable).where(eq(dealsTable.id, id)).limit(1);
  if (!deal) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Сделка не найдена" }); return; }
  if (!["open", "declined"].includes(deal.status)) {
    res.status(409).json({ error: `Нельзя отменить сделку в статусе «${deal.status}»` });
    return;
  }
  if (deal.senderId !== parsed.data.citizenId) { res.status(403).json({ error: "Только отправитель может отменить сделку" }); return; }

  // Return escrow to sender
  const [sender] = await db
    .select({ balance: citizensTable.balanceMinutes })
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, deal.senderId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!sender) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Отправитель не найден" }); return; }

  await db
    .update(citizensTable)
    .set({ balanceMinutes: sender.balance + deal.amount })
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, deal.senderId));

  const [updated] = await db
    .update(dealsTable)
    .set({ status: "cancelled", updatedAt: new Date() })
    .where(eq(dealsTable.id, id))
    .returning();

  req.log.info({ dealId: id, senderId: deal.senderId, refunded: deal.amount }, "Deal cancelled, escrow returned to sender");
  res.json(updated);
});

// ─── POST /api/deals/:id/dispute ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// Either party flags the deal for manual resolution.
router.post("/deals/:id/dispute", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params["id"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(id)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid id" }); return; }

  const parsed = ActorBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: "citizenId обязателен" }); return; }

  const [deal] = await db.select().from(dealsTable).where(eq(dealsTable.id, id)).limit(1);
  if (!deal) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Сделка не найдена" }); return; }
  if (!["accepted", "proof_submitted", "declined"].includes(deal.status)) {
    res.status(409).json({ error: "Спор можно открыть только для активной сделки" });
    return;
  }
  if (deal.senderId !== parsed.data.citizenId && deal.receiverId !== parsed.data.citizenId) {
    res.status(403).json({ error: "Только участник сделки может открыть спор" });
    return;
  }

  const [updated] = await db
    .update(dealsTable)
    .set({ status: "disputed", updatedAt: new Date() })
    .where(eq(dealsTable.id, id))
    .returning();

  req.log.info({ dealId: id, initiatorId: parsed.data.citizenId }, "Deal disputed");
  res.json(updated);
});

export default router;

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/chronopedia.ts ---
import { Router } from "express";
import { z } from "zod/v4";
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import {
  chronopediaSlotsTable,
  chronopediaEventsTable,
  citizensTable,
} from "@workspace/db";
import { eq, ne, desc, and, sql } from "drizzle-orm";

const router = Router();

// ── Validation schemas ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const CreateSlotBody = z.object({
  citizenId: z.number().int().positive(),
  geoLat: z.number().min(-90).max(90),
  geoLng: z.number().min(-180).max(180),
  locationName: z.string().min(1).max(200),
  placeId: z.string().min(1).max(300).optional(),
  timeMarker: z.string().min(1).max(100),
  title: z.string().min(1).max(200),
  description: z.string().min(1).max(2000),
});

const AddEventBody = z.object({
  citizenId: z.number().int().positive(),
  title: z.string().min(1).max(200),
  description: z.string().min(1).max(2000),
});

const EditEventBody = z.object({
  citizenId: z.number().int().positive(),
  title: z.string().min(1).max(200).optional(),
  description: z.string().min(1).max(2000).optional(),
});

// Generate synthetic placeId from coordinates (±1km grid)
function syntheticPlaceId(lat: number, lng: number): string {
  const rLat = Math.round(lat * 100) / 100;
  const rLng = Math.round(lng * 100) / 100;
  return `syn_${rLat}_${rLng}`;
}

// ── GET /api/chronopedia/slots?citizenId=X ────────────────────────────────────
// All slots with event counts and hasMyEvent flag.
router.get("/chronopedia/slots", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const citizenId = parseInt(String(req.query["citizenId"] ?? ""), 10) || null;

  const rows = await db
    .select({
      id: chronopediaSlotsTable.id,
      timeMarker: chronopediaSlotsTable.timeMarker,
      placeId: chronopediaSlotsTable.placeId,
      geoLat: chronopediaSlotsTable.geoLat,
      geoLng: chronopediaSlotsTable.geoLng,
      locationName: chronopediaSlotsTable.locationName,
      createdAt: chronopediaSlotsTable.createdAt,
      eventCount: sql<number>`count(case when ${chronopediaEventsTable.status} != 'deleted' then 1 end)::int`,
      hasMyEvent: citizenId
        ? sql<boolean>`bool_or(${chronopediaEventsTable.authorId} = ${citizenId} and ${chronopediaEventsTable.status} != 'deleted')`
        : sql<boolean>`false`,
    })
    .from(chronopediaSlotsTable)
    .leftJoin(
      chronopediaEventsTable,
      eq(chronopediaEventsTable.slotId, chronopediaSlotsTable.id)
    )
    .groupBy(chronopediaSlotsTable.id)
    .orderBy(desc(chronopediaSlotsTable.createdAt))
    .limit(500);

  res.json({ slots: rows });
});

// ── GET /api/chronopedia/feed?citizenId=X&limit=50 ───────────────────────────
// Recent events across all slots (for feed view).
router.get("/chronopedia/feed", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const limit = Math.min(parseInt(String(req.query["limit"] ?? "50"), 10) || 50, 100);

  const events = await db
    .select({
      id: chronopediaEventsTable.id,
      slotId: chronopediaEventsTable.slotId,
      authorId: chronopediaEventsTable.authorId,
      authorName: citizensTable.name,
      authorRank: citizensTable.rank,
      title: chronopediaEventsTable.title,
      description: chronopediaEventsTable.description,
      status: chronopediaEventsTable.status,
      createdAt: chronopediaEventsTable.createdAt,
      locationName: chronopediaSlotsTable.locationName,
      timeMarker: chronopediaSlotsTable.timeMarker,
      geoLat: chronopediaSlotsTable.geoLat,
      geoLng: chronopediaSlotsTable.geoLng,
    })
    .from(chronopediaEventsTable)
    .innerJoin(
      chronopediaSlotsTable,
      eq(chronopediaSlotsTable.id, chronopediaEventsTable.slotId)
    )
    .innerJoin(citizensTable, eq(citizensTable.id, chronopediaEventsTable.authorId))
    .where(ne(chronopediaEventsTable.status, "deleted"))
    .orderBy(desc(chronopediaEventsTable.createdAt))
    .limit(limit);

  res.json({ events });
});

// ── GET /api/chronopedia/slots/:id ───────────────────────────────────────────
// Slot detail with all non-deleted events + author info.
router.get("/chronopedia/slots/:id", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const slotId = parseInt(req.params["id"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(slotId)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid slot ID" }); return; }

  const [slot] = await db
    .select()
    .from(chronopediaSlotsTable)
    .where(eq(chronopediaSlotsTable.id, slotId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!slot) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Слот не найден" }); return; }

  const events = await db
    .select({
      id: chronopediaEventsTable.id,
      authorId: chronopediaEventsTable.authorId,
      authorName: citizensTable.name,
      authorRank: citizensTable.rank,
      title: chronopediaEventsTable.title,
      description: chronopediaEventsTable.description,
      status: chronopediaEventsTable.status,
      createdAt: chronopediaEventsTable.createdAt,
      updatedAt: chronopediaEventsTable.updatedAt,
    })
    .from(chronopediaEventsTable)
    .innerJoin(citizensTable, eq(citizensTable.id, chronopediaEventsTable.authorId))
    .where(
      and(
        eq(chronopediaEventsTable.slotId, slotId),
        ne(chronopediaEventsTable.status, "deleted")
      )
    )
    .orderBy(desc(chronopediaEventsTable.createdAt));

  res.json({ slot, events });
});

// ── POST /api/chronopedia/slots ───────────────────────────────────────────────
// Create a new space-time slot + first opinion.
// If slot with same timeMarker+placeId already exists → adds event to existing slot.
router.post("/chronopedia/slots", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const parsed = CreateSlotBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message }); return; }

  const { citizenId, geoLat, geoLng, locationName, timeMarker, title, description } = parsed.data;
  const placeId = parsed.data.placeId ?? syntheticPlaceId(geoLat, geoLng);

  const [citizen] = await db
    .select({ id: citizensTable.id })
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Гражданин не найден" }); return; }

  let slotId: number;
  let created = false;

  try {
    const [inserted] = await db
      .insert(chronopediaSlotsTable)
      .values({ timeMarker, placeId, geoLat, geoLng, locationName })
      .returning({ id: chronopediaSlotsTable.id });
    slotId = inserted!.id;
    created = true;
  } catch {
    // Unique constraint violation — slot already exists, find it
    const [existing] = await db
      .select({ id: chronopediaSlotsTable.id })
      .from(chronopediaSlotsTable)
      .where(
        and(
          eq(chronopediaSlotsTable.timeMarker, timeMarker),
          eq(chronopediaSlotsTable.placeId, placeId)
        )
      )
      .limit(1);

    if (!existing) { res.status(409).json({ error: "Конфликт при создании слота" }); return; }
    slotId = existing.id;
  }

  const [event] = await db
    .insert(chronopediaEventsTable)
    .values({ slotId, authorId: citizenId, title, description, status: "approved" })
    .returning({ id: chronopediaEventsTable.id });

  req.log.info({ citizenId, slotId, created }, "Chronopedia slot/event created");
  res.status(201).json({ ok: true, slotId, eventId: event!.id, created });
});

// ── POST /api/chronopedia/slots/:id/events ────────────────────────────────────
// Add an opinion to an existing slot. Free — no Дармин cost.
router.post("/chronopedia/slots/:id/events", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const slotId = parseInt(req.params["id"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(slotId)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid slot ID" }); return; }

  const parsed = AddEventBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message }); return; }

  const { citizenId, title, description } = parsed.data;

  const [slot] = await db
    .select({ id: chronopediaSlotsTable.id })
    .from(chronopediaSlotsTable)
    .where(eq(chronopediaSlotsTable.id, slotId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!slot) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Слот не найден" }); return; }

  const [citizen] = await db
    .select({ id: citizensTable.id })
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Гражданин не найден" }); return; }

  const [event] = await db
    .insert(chronopediaEventsTable)
    .values({ slotId, authorId: citizenId, title, description, status: "approved" })
    .returning({ id: chronopediaEventsTable.id });

  req.log.info({ citizenId, slotId }, "Chronopedia event added");
  res.status(201).json({ ok: true, eventId: event!.id });
});

// ── PATCH /api/chronopedia/events/:id ────────────────────────────────────────
// Edit own event (title and/or description).
router.patch("/chronopedia/events/:id", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const eventId = parseInt(req.params["id"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(eventId)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid event ID" }); return; }

  const parsed = EditEventBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error.message }); return; }

  const { citizenId, title, description } = parsed.data;

  const [event] = await db
    .select({ id: chronopediaEventsTable.id, authorId: chronopediaEventsTable.authorId })
    .from(chronopediaEventsTable)
    .where(eq(chronopediaEventsTable.id, eventId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!event) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Запись не найдена" }); return; }
  if (event.authorId !== citizenId) {
    res.status(403).json({ error: "Только автор может редактировать запись" });
    return;
  }

  const updates: Record<string, unknown> = { updatedAt: new Date() };
  if (title !== undefined) updates["title"] = title;
  if (description !== undefined) updates["description"] = description;

  await db
    .update(chronopediaEventsTable)
    .set(updates)
    .where(eq(chronopediaEventsTable.id, eventId));

  res.json({ ok: true });
});

// ── DELETE /api/chronopedia/events/:id ───────────────────────────────────────
// Soft-delete own event (sets status = 'deleted').
router.delete("/chronopedia/events/:id", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const eventId = parseInt(req.params["id"] ?? "", 10);
  if (isNaN(eventId)) { res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid event ID" }); return; }

  const citizenId = z.number().int().positive().safeParse(req.body?.citizenId);
  if (!citizenId.success) { res.status(400).json({ error: "citizenId обязателен" }); return; }

  const [event] = await db
    .select({ id: chronopediaEventsTable.id, authorId: chronopediaEventsTable.authorId })
    .from(chronopediaEventsTable)
    .where(eq(chronopediaEventsTable.id, eventId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!event) { res.status(404).json({ error: "Запись не найдена" }); return; }
  if (event.authorId !== citizenId.data) {
    res.status(403).json({ error: "Только автор может удалить запись" });
    return;
  }

  await db
    .update(chronopediaEventsTable)
    .set({ status: "deleted", updatedAt: new Date() })
    .where(eq(chronopediaEventsTable.id, eventId));

  res.json({ ok: true });
});

// ── GET /api/chronopedia/places/search?q=TEXT ────────────────────────────────
// Proxy to Google Places Text Search API (keeps key server-side).
router.get("/chronopedia/places/search", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const q = String(req.query["q"] ?? "").trim();
  if (!q) { res.json({ places: [] }); return; }

  const apiKey = process.env["GOOGLE_API_KEY"];
  if (!apiKey) { res.json({ places: [] }); return; }

  try {
    const url = `https://maps.googleapis.com/maps/api/place/textsearch/json?query=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&key=${apiKey}&language=ru`;
    const upstream = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) });
    if (!upstream.ok) { res.json({ places: [] }); return; }

    const data = await upstream.json() as {
      results?: {
        place_id: string;
        name: string;
        formatted_address: string;
        geometry: { location: { lat: number; lng: number } };
      }[];
    };

    const places = (data.results ?? []).slice(0, 5).map((r) => ({
      placeId: r.place_id,
      name: r.name,
      address: r.formatted_address,
      lat: r.geometry.location.lat,
      lng: r.geometry.location.lng,
    }));

    res.json({ places });
  } catch {
    res.json({ places: [] });
  }
});

export default router;

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/services/emissionService.ts ---
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { emissionLogsTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { eq, gte, lte, sum, and, isNotNull, isNull } from "drizzle-orm";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";

// Суточный лимит эмиссии Дарминов по всей сети
export const DAILY_EMISSION_LIMIT = 350;

function todayUTC(): Date {
  const d = new Date();
  d.setUTCHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  return d;
}

function tomorrowUTC(): Date {
  const d = todayUTC();
  d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + 1);
  return d;
}

/** Суммарная одобренная эмиссия за сегодня (UTC) */
export async function getDailyApprovedTotal(): Promise<number> {
  const [row] = await db
    .select({ total: sum(emissionLogsTable.amount) })
    .from(emissionLogsTable)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(emissionLogsTable.isApproved, true),
        gte(emissionLogsTable.createdAt, todayUTC()),
      )
    );
  return Number(row?.total ?? 0);
}

/** Размер FIFO-очереди (записи, ожидающие следующего дня) */
export async function getQueueSize(): Promise<number> {
  const rows = await db
    .select({ id: emissionLogsTable.id })
    .from(emissionLogsTable)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(emissionLogsTable.isApproved, false),
        isNotNull(emissionLogsTable.scheduledFor),
      )
    );
  return rows.length;
}

/**
 * Запросить эмиссию Дарминов для гражданина.
 * Если дневной лимит исчерпан — записывает в FIFO-очередь (isApproved=false, scheduledFor=следующий 00:00 UTC).
 * Возвращает { approved: true } если зачислено немедленно, { approved: false, scheduledFor } если встало в очередь.
 */
export async function requestEmission(
  citizenId: number,
  amount: number,
  reason: string
): Promise<{ approved: boolean; scheduledFor?: Date }> {
  const currentTotal = await getDailyApprovedTotal();

  if (currentTotal + amount <= DAILY_EMISSION_LIMIT) {
    await db.insert(emissionLogsTable).values({
      citizenId,
      amount,
      reason,
      dailyTotal: currentTotal + amount,
      isApproved: true,
      scheduledFor: null,
    });
    logger.info({ citizenId, amount, reason, dailyTotal: currentTotal + amount }, "Emission approved");
    return { approved: true };
  }

  const scheduledFor = tomorrowUTC();
  await db.insert(emissionLogsTable).values({
    citizenId,
    amount,
    reason,
    dailyTotal: currentTotal,
    isApproved: false,
    scheduledFor,
  });
  logger.warn(
    { citizenId, amount, reason, currentTotal, scheduledFor },
    "Emission queued (daily limit reached)"
  );
  return { approved: false, scheduledFor };
}

/**
 * Cron-задача 00:00 UTC — дрейнировать FIFO-очередь.
 * Одобряет pending-записи, пока не упрётся в лимит нового дня.
 * Возвращает количество обработанных записей.
 */
export async function drainEmissionQueue(): Promise<number> {
  const pending = await db
    .select()
    .from(emissionLogsTable)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(emissionLogsTable.isApproved, false),
        isNotNull(emissionLogsTable.scheduledFor),
        lte(emissionLogsTable.scheduledFor!, new Date()),
      )
    )
    .orderBy(emissionLogsTable.scheduledFor);

  if (pending.length === 0) return 0;

  let processed = 0;
  let runningTotal = 0;

  for (const entry of pending) {
    if (runningTotal + entry.amount > DAILY_EMISSION_LIMIT) break;

    await db
      .update(emissionLogsTable)
      .set({ isApproved: true, dailyTotal: runningTotal + entry.amount })
      .where(eq(emissionLogsTable.id, entry.id));

    runningTotal += entry.amount;
    processed++;
  }

  logger.info({ processed, newDailyTotal: runningTotal }, "Emission queue drained");
  return processed;
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/services/meritocracyService.ts ---
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { citizensTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { eq, lt, or, isNull } from "drizzle-orm";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";
import { sendTransactionNotification } from "./telegramService";

// ── Single Source of Truth for ranks ─────────────────────────────────────────

export const RANKS = [
  { threshold: 0,    name: "Гражданин" },
  { threshold: 100,  name: "Оператор времени" },
  { threshold: 500,  name: "Хранитель времени" },
  { threshold: 1000, name: "Архитектор потока" },
  { threshold: 2500, name: "Мастер хронологии" },
  { threshold: 5000, name: "Синхронизатор вечности" },
] as const;

export type RankName = (typeof RANKS)[number]["name"];

/**
 * Calculates the rank for a given merit score.
 * Iterates from highest threshold downward and returns the first match.
 */
export function calculateRank(meritScore: number): RankName {
  for (let i = RANKS.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    if (meritScore >= RANKS[i]!.threshold) return RANKS[i]!.name;
  }
  return RANKS[0]!.name;
}

/** @deprecated Use calculateRank() */
export const getUserRank = calculateRank;

export function getRankIndex(rankName: string): number {
  return RANKS.findIndex((r) => r.name === rankName);
}

// ── Merit gain formula ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

interface MeritGainParams {
  minutesTransferred: number;
  dailyMinutesTotal: number;
  daysSinceRegistration: number;
}

export function calculateMeritGain(params: MeritGainParams): number {
  const { minutesTransferred, dailyMinutesTotal, daysSinceRegistration } = params;

  let merit = 1;
  merit += Math.floor(minutesTransferred / 10) * 0.1;
  if (dailyMinutesTotal > 60) merit += 5;
  const loyaltyCoeff = 1 + daysSinceRegistration / 100;
  merit = merit * loyaltyCoeff;

  return Math.round(merit * 100) / 100;
}

// ── Apply merit after a TiMETR session ────────────────────────────────────────

export async function applyMeritAfterSession(
  citizenId: number,
  minutesTransferred: number
): Promise<{ oldRank: string; newRank: string; meritGained: number; newScore: number }> {
  const [citizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) throw new Error(`Citizen ${citizenId} not found`);

  const now = new Date();
  const daysSinceRegistration = Math.floor(
    (now.getTime() - citizen.createdAt.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)
  );

  const meritGained = calculateMeritGain({
    minutesTransferred,
    dailyMinutesTotal: citizen.totalGiven,
    daysSinceRegistration,
  });

  const oldRank = citizen.rank;
  const newScore = Math.round((citizen.meritScore + meritGained) * 100) / 100;
  const newRank = calculateRank(newScore);

  await db
    .update(citizensTable)
    .set({ meritScore: newScore, rank: newRank, lastActivity: now })
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId));

  logger.info(
    { citizenId, oldRank, newRank, meritGained, newScore },
    "Merit applied after session"
  );

  return { oldRank, newRank, meritGained, newScore };
}

// ── Weekly entropy (−0.5 merit for inactive citizens) ─────────────────────────

export async function applyEntropy(): Promise<void> {
  const sevenDaysAgo = new Date(Date.now() - 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);

  const inactiveCitizens = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(
      or(
        isNull(citizensTable.lastActivity),
        lt(citizensTable.lastActivity, sevenDaysAgo)
      )
    );

  logger.info({ count: inactiveCitizens.length }, "Applying entropy to inactive citizens");

  for (const citizen of inactiveCitizens) {
    if (citizen.meritScore <= 0) continue;

    const oldRank = citizen.rank;
    const newScore = Math.max(0, Math.round((citizen.meritScore - 0.5) * 100) / 100);
    const newRank = calculateRank(newScore);

    await db
      .update(citizensTable)
      .set({ meritScore: newScore, rank: newRank })
      .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizen.id));

    if (newRank !== oldRank && getRankIndex(newRank) < getRankIndex(oldRank)) {
      await sendTransactionNotification(
        citizen.id,
        `⚠️ *Казначейство TimeNation*\n\n` +
        `Внимание! Энтропия затронула ваш профиль.\n` +
        `Ваш статус понижен до *${newRank}*.\n\n` +
        `Проведите TiMETR-сессию, чтобы вернуть влияние.`
      ).catch(() => {});

      logger.info(
        { citizenId: citizen.id, oldRank, newRank },
        "Entropy rank downgrade"
      );
    }
  }
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/services/timeBankService.ts ---
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { timeBankLoansTable, timeBankLedgerTable, citizensTable, transactionsTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { eq, sum, count, and, asc } from "drizzle-orm";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";

export const MAX_DEBT_CAP_MINUTES = 480; // 8 часов — абсолютный потолок

/**
 * Лестница доверия:
 * 0 услуг → 60 мин
 * 1+ услуга, <1 ч total → 120 мин
 * 2 ч total → 180 мин, 3 ч → 240 мин … 7 ч → 480 мин (cap)
 */
export function calculateMaxCreditLimit(
  completedServicesCount: number,
  totalMinutesWorked: number
): number {
  if (completedServicesCount === 0) return 60;
  const hoursWorked = Math.floor(totalMinutesWorked / 60);
  const step = Math.max(2, hoursWorked + 1); // минимум шаг 2 (120 мин) при 1+ услуге
  return Math.min(step * 60, MAX_DEBT_CAP_MINUTES);
}

/** Уровень, необходимый для следующего шага */
export function nextLevelRequirement(
  completedServicesCount: number,
  totalMinutesWorked: number
): { label: string; currentMinutes: number; targetMinutes: number } | null {
  const currentLimit = calculateMaxCreditLimit(completedServicesCount, totalMinutesWorked);
  if (currentLimit >= MAX_DEBT_CAP_MINUTES) return null; // уже потолок
  if (completedServicesCount === 0) {
    return { label: "Выполните первую услугу", currentMinutes: 0, targetMinutes: 0 };
  }
  const hoursWorked = Math.floor(totalMinutesWorked / 60);
  const nextHours = Math.max(2, hoursWorked + 1);
  return {
    label: `Отработайте ${nextHours} ч для лимита ${(nextHours + 1) * 60} мин`,
    currentMinutes: totalMinutesWorked,
    targetMinutes: nextHours * 60,
  };
}

/** Суммарный активный долг гражданина (Д) */
export async function getActiveDebt(citizenId: number): Promise<number> {
  const [row] = await db
    .select({ total: sum(timeBankLoansTable.remainingDebt) })
    .from(timeBankLoansTable)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(timeBankLoansTable.citizenId, citizenId),
        eq(timeBankLoansTable.status, "active")
      )
    );
  return Number(row?.total ?? 0);
}

/** Статус Банка Времени для гражданина */
export async function getTimeBankStatus(citizenId: number) {
  const [citizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) throw new Error("Гражданин не найден");

  const [svcRow] = await db
    .select({ cnt: count() })
    .from(transactionsTable)
    .where(eq(transactionsTable.toCitizenId, citizenId));

  const completedServicesCount = Number(svcRow?.cnt ?? 0);
  const totalMinutesWorked = citizen.totalReceived;

  const creditLimit = calculateMaxCreditLimit(completedServicesCount, totalMinutesWorked);
  const activeDebt = await getActiveDebt(citizenId);
  const availableCredit = Math.max(0, creditLimit - activeDebt);

  const nextLevel = nextLevelRequirement(completedServicesCount, totalMinutesWorked);
  const atCap = creditLimit >= MAX_DEBT_CAP_MINUTES;

  const loans = await db
    .select()
    .from(timeBankLoansTable)
    .where(eq(timeBankLoansTable.citizenId, citizenId));

  const ledger = await db
    .select()
    .from(timeBankLedgerTable)
    .where(eq(timeBankLedgerTable.citizenId, citizenId))
    .orderBy(asc(timeBankLedgerTable.createdAt));

  return {
    citizenId,
    creditLimit,
    activeDebt,
    availableCredit,
    maxDebtCap: MAX_DEBT_CAP_MINUTES,
    atCap,
    completedServicesCount,
    totalMinutesWorked,
    nextLevel,
    statusLabel: atCap
      ? "Достигнут максимальный предел доверия Банка Времени (8 часов)"
      : `Лимит доверия: ${creditLimit} мин`,
    loans: loans.map((l) => ({
      ...l,
      createdAt: l.createdAt.toISOString(),
      repaidAt: l.repaidAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
    })),
    ledger: ledger.map((e) => ({
      ...e,
      createdAt: e.createdAt.toISOString(),
    })),
  };
}

/** Взять кредит в Банке Времени */
export async function borrowFromBank(citizenId: number, amount: number): Promise<void> {
  if (amount <= 0) throw new Error("Сумма кредита должна быть положительной");

  const [citizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) throw new Error("Гражданин не найден");

  const [svcRow] = await db
    .select({ cnt: count() })
    .from(transactionsTable)
    .where(eq(transactionsTable.toCitizenId, citizenId));

  const completedServicesCount = Number(svcRow?.cnt ?? 0);
  const creditLimit = calculateMaxCreditLimit(completedServicesCount, citizen.totalReceived);
  const activeDebt = await getActiveDebt(citizenId);

  if (activeDebt + amount > creditLimit) {
    const available = Math.max(0, creditLimit - activeDebt);
    if (available === 0 && creditLimit >= MAX_DEBT_CAP_MINUTES) {
      throw new Error(
        "Достигнут максимальный предел доверия Банка Времени (8 часов). Погасите долг для дальнейшего кредитования."
      );
    }
    throw new Error(
      `Превышен кредитный лимит. Доступно: ${available} мин (лимит ${creditLimit} мин). ` +
      `Выполните больше услуг для повышения лимита.`
    );
  }

  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
    const [loan] = await tx.insert(timeBankLoansTable).values({
      citizenId,
      amount,
      remainingDebt: amount,
      status: "active",
    }).returning();

    await tx.insert(timeBankLedgerTable).values({
      citizenId,
      loanId: loan!.id,
      type: "loan_issued",
      amount,
      note: `Выдан кредит ${amount} Д`,
    });

    await tx
      .update(citizensTable)
      .set({ balanceMinutes: citizen.balanceMinutes + amount })
      .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId));
  });

  logger.info({ citizenId, amount, creditLimit }, "Банк Времени: выдан кредит");
}

/**
 * Ручное погашение долга (FIFO по активным займам).
 * Списывает с баланса, создаёт ledger-записи, освобождает залог при обнулении долга.
 */
export async function repayFromBalance(citizenId: number, amount: number): Promise<void> {
  if (amount <= 0) throw new Error("Сумма погашения должна быть положительной");

  const [citizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) throw new Error("Гражданин не найден");

  const transferable = citizen.balanceMinutes - 1; // родовая минута неприкосновенна
  if (transferable < amount) {
    throw new Error(`Недостаточно минут на балансе. Доступно для погашения: ${transferable} Д`);
  }

  const activeLoans = await db
    .select()
    .from(timeBankLoansTable)
    .where(and(eq(timeBankLoansTable.citizenId, citizenId), eq(timeBankLoansTable.status, "active")))
    .orderBy(asc(timeBankLoansTable.createdAt));

  if (activeLoans.length === 0) throw new Error("Нет активных займов для погашения");

  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
    let remaining = amount;

    for (const loan of activeLoans) {
      if (remaining <= 0) break;
      const toDeduct = Math.min(remaining, loan.remainingDebt);
      const newRemaining = loan.remainingDebt - toDeduct;
      const fullyRepaid = newRemaining === 0;

      await tx
        .update(timeBankLoansTable)
        .set({
          remainingDebt: newRemaining,
          status: fullyRepaid ? "repaid" : "active",
          repaidAt: fullyRepaid ? new Date() : undefined,
        })
        .where(eq(timeBankLoansTable.id, loan.id));

      await tx.insert(timeBankLedgerTable).values({
        citizenId,
        loanId: loan.id,
        type: "manual_repayment",
        amount: toDeduct,
        note: `Ручное погашение ${toDeduct} Д`,
      });

      if (fullyRepaid) {
        await tx.insert(timeBankLedgerTable).values({
          citizenId,
          loanId: loan.id,
          type: "collateral_released",
          amount: loan.amount,
          note: `Залог освобождён: ${loan.amount} Д`,
        });
      }

      remaining -= toDeduct;
    }

    // Списываем только фактически погашенное (amount - remaining),
    // а не весь запрошенный amount — защита от переплаты если долг меньше суммы.
    const actualRepaid = amount - remaining;
    await tx
      .update(citizensTable)
      .set({ balanceMinutes: citizen.balanceMinutes - actualRepaid })
      .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId));
  });

  logger.info({ citizenId, amount }, "Банк Времени: ручное погашение");
}

/**
 * Автоматическое погашение долга из заработанных минут (вызывается при завершении TiMETR-сессии).
 * НЕ обновляет citizens.balance_minutes — это делает вызывающий код.
 * Возвращает сумму, направленную на погашение долга (вычитается из зачисляемых минут).
 */
export async function autoRepayFromService(
  citizenId: number,
  earnedMinutes: number
): Promise<number> {
  if (earnedMinutes <= 0) return 0;

  const activeLoans = await db
    .select()
    .from(timeBankLoansTable)
    .where(and(eq(timeBankLoansTable.citizenId, citizenId), eq(timeBankLoansTable.status, "active")))
    .orderBy(asc(timeBankLoansTable.createdAt));

  if (activeLoans.length === 0) return 0;

  let totalAutoRepaid = 0;
  let remaining = earnedMinutes;

  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
    for (const loan of activeLoans) {
      if (remaining <= 0) break;
      const toDeduct = Math.min(remaining, loan.remainingDebt);
      const newRemaining = loan.remainingDebt - toDeduct;
      const fullyRepaid = newRemaining === 0;

      await tx
        .update(timeBankLoansTable)
        .set({
          remainingDebt: newRemaining,
          status: fullyRepaid ? "repaid" : "active",
          repaidAt: fullyRepaid ? new Date() : undefined,
        })
        .where(eq(timeBankLoansTable.id, loan.id));

      await tx.insert(timeBankLedgerTable).values({
        citizenId,
        loanId: loan.id,
        type: "auto_repayment",
        amount: toDeduct,
        note: `Автопогашение из TiMETR: ${toDeduct} Д`,
      });

      if (fullyRepaid) {
        await tx.insert(timeBankLedgerTable).values({
          citizenId,
          loanId: loan.id,
          type: "collateral_released",
          amount: loan.amount,
          note: `Залог освобождён: ${loan.amount} Д`,
        });
      }

      totalAutoRepaid += toDeduct;
      remaining -= toDeduct;
    }
  });

  if (totalAutoRepaid > 0) {
    logger.info({ citizenId, earnedMinutes, totalAutoRepaid }, "Банк Времени: автопогашение из TiMETR");
  }

  return totalAutoRepaid;
}

/** @deprecated Используй repayFromBalance (FIFO, без loanId) */
export async function repayLoan(citizenId: number, loanId: number, amount: number): Promise<void> {
  const [loan] = await db
    .select()
    .from(timeBankLoansTable)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(timeBankLoansTable.id, loanId),
        eq(timeBankLoansTable.citizenId, citizenId),
        eq(timeBankLoansTable.status, "active")
      )
    )
    .limit(1);

  if (!loan) throw new Error("Кредит не найден или уже погашен");

  const repayAmount = Math.min(amount, loan.remainingDebt);

  const [citizen] = await db
    .select()
    .from(citizensTable)
    .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!citizen) throw new Error("Гражданин не найден");

  const transferable = citizen.balanceMinutes - 1;
  if (transferable < repayAmount) {
    throw new Error(`Недостаточно Дарминов для погашения. Доступно: ${transferable} Д`);
  }

  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
    const newRemaining = loan.remainingDebt - repayAmount;
    const fullyRepaid = newRemaining === 0;

    await tx
      .update(timeBankLoansTable)
      .set({
        remainingDebt: newRemaining,
        status: fullyRepaid ? "repaid" : "active",
        repaidAt: fullyRepaid ? new Date() : undefined,
      })
      .where(eq(timeBankLoansTable.id, loanId));

    await tx.insert(timeBankLedgerTable).values({
      citizenId,
      loanId,
      type: "manual_repayment",
      amount: repayAmount,
      note: `Ручное погашение ${repayAmount} Д`,
    });

    if (fullyRepaid) {
      await tx.insert(timeBankLedgerTable).values({
        citizenId,
        loanId,
        type: "collateral_released",
        amount: loan.amount,
        note: `Залог освобождён: ${loan.amount} Д`,
      });
    }

    await tx
      .update(citizensTable)
      .set({ balanceMinutes: citizen.balanceMinutes - repayAmount })
      .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId));
  });

  logger.info({ citizenId, loanId, repayAmount }, "Банк Времени: погашение долга");
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/services/tvService.ts ---
import crypto from "crypto";
import type { Request } from "express";
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { tvSlotsTable, tvCapsulesTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { and, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";
import { sendTransactionNotification } from "./telegramService";

// ── Slot type by minute position ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// apex    : minuteIndex % 60 === 0           (:00)  → 24/day
// half    : minuteIndex % 30 === 0, ≠ :00    (:30)  → 24/day
// quarter : minuteIndex % 15 === 0, ≠ :00/:30 (:15/:45) → 48/day
// citizen : all others                                 → 1344/day
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export type SlotType = "citizen" | "quarter" | "half" | "apex";

export function getSlotType(minuteIndex: number): SlotType {
  const m = minuteIndex % 60;
  if (m === 0) return "apex";
  if (m === 30) return "half";
  if (m % 15 === 0) return "quarter";
  return "citizen";
}

// ── Burn Matrix ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export const BURN_MATRIX: Record<
  SlotType,
  { eur: number; priceEurCents: number; timeltr: number; label: string }
> = {
  citizen: { eur: 4.99,  priceEurCents: 499,  timeltr: 1,  label: "Citizen Minute" },
  quarter: { eur: 9.99,  priceEurCents: 999,  timeltr: 10, label: "Quarter Minute (:15/:45)" },
  half:    { eur: 24.99, priceEurCents: 2499, timeltr: 25, label: "Half Minute (:30)" },
  apex:    { eur: 49.99, priceEurCents: 4999, timeltr: 50, label: "Apex Minute (:00)" },
};

// ── Fingerprint for anti-consecutive guard ────────────────────────────────────
export function getFingerprint(req: Request): string {
  const raw = `${req.ip ?? "unknown"}:${req.headers["user-agent"] ?? ""}`;
  return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(raw).digest("hex");
}

// ── Sovereign URL ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export function generateSovereignUrl(date: string, minuteIndex: number): string {
  const hour = String(Math.floor(minuteIndex / 60)).padStart(2, "0");
  const min = String(minuteIndex % 60).padStart(2, "0");
  return `timenation.tv/moment/${date}-${hour}${min}`;
}

// ── Retro detection (slot_time < NOW) ─────────────────────────────────────────
export function isRetroSlot(date: string, minuteIndex: number): boolean {
  const hour = Math.floor(minuteIndex / 60);
  const min = minuteIndex % 60;
  const slotTime = new Date(
    `${date}T${String(hour).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(min).padStart(2, "0")}:00Z`
  );
  return slotTime < new Date();
}

// ── Date validation helper ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export function isValidDate(date: string): boolean {
  return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(date) && !isNaN(Date.parse(date));
}

// ── Generate all 1440 slots for a given date (idempotent) ────────────────────
export async function ensureSlotsForDate(date: string): Promise<void> {
  const [existing] = await db
    .select({ id: tvSlotsTable.id })
    .from(tvSlotsTable)
    .where(eq(tvSlotsTable.date, date))
    .limit(1);

  if (existing) return; // Already generated

  const rows = Array.from({ length: 1440 }, (_, i) => {
    const slotType = getSlotType(i);
    const pricing = BURN_MATRIX[slotType];
    return {
      date,
      minuteIndex: i,
      slotType,
      status: "free" as const,
      priceEur: pricing.eur,
      timeltrBurnCost: pricing.timeltr,
    };
  });

  // Insert in chunks of 200 to avoid large parameter arrays
  for (let offset = 0; offset < rows.length; offset += 200) {
    await db
      .insert(tvSlotsTable)
      .values(rows.slice(offset, offset + 200))
      .onConflictDoNothing();
  }

  logger.info({ date, count: 1440 }, "TV slots generated for date");
}

// ── Air Due Slots (runs every minute) ─────────────────────────────────────────
// Finds booked slots whose date+minuteIndex matches the current UTC minute,
// marks them "broadcast", marks their capsule "broadcast", notifies owner.
export async function airDueSlots(): Promise<number> {
  const now = new Date();
  const todayUTC = now.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const minuteIndex = now.getUTCHours() * 60 + now.getUTCMinutes();

  const dueSlots = await db
    .select()
    .from(tvSlotsTable)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(tvSlotsTable.date, todayUTC),
        eq(tvSlotsTable.minuteIndex, minuteIndex),
        eq(tvSlotsTable.status, "booked"),
      )
    );

  if (dueSlots.length === 0) return 0;

  for (const slot of dueSlots) {
    await db
      .update(tvSlotsTable)
      .set({ status: "broadcast" })
      .where(eq(tvSlotsTable.id, slot.id));

    await db
      .update(tvCapsulesTable)
      .set({ status: "broadcast" })
      .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slot.id))
      .catch(() => {});

    if (slot.ownerId) {
      const hh = String(Math.floor(minuteIndex / 60)).padStart(2, "0");
      const mm = String(minuteIndex % 60).padStart(2, "0");
      sendTransactionNotification(
        slot.ownerId,
        `📡 *Ваш слот в эфире!*\n\n🗓 ${todayUTC} · ${hh}:${mm} UTC\nТип: ${slot.slotType}\n\nВаша капсула вышла в эфир TimeNation-TV.`,
      ).catch(() => {});
    }
  }

  logger.info({ todayUTC, minuteIndex, count: dueSlots.length }, "Slots aired");
  return dueSlots.length;
}
