// ── Mode A: Template Manifesto Expansion (~130 words, 60-second fallback) ─────
// Used when Gemini is unavailable. Approx 120–140 words = 60 sec at natural pace.
export function expandSeedToManifesto(seed: string): string {
  const clean = seed.trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
  const idx = Math.abs(hashStr(clean)) % 3;

  const openings = [
    `Я говорю это прямо сейчас, в одну из 1440 минут, из которых состоят сутки. Моя мысль: «${clean}».`,
    `В глобальном эфире TimeNation каждая минута — координата. Сегодня моя: «${clean}».`,
    `Мир вещает непрерывно. Я выбираю свою минуту, чтобы сказать: «${clean}».`,
  ];

  const bodies = [
    `Эту минуту нельзя стереть, купить задним числом или подделать. Она выкуплена, зафиксирована в сети. Дармин сгорел — но координата осталась навсегда. Глобальное вещание — это не привилегия корпораций. Это право каждого гражданина, у которого есть что сказать миру прямо сейчас.`,
    `1440 слотов в сутках. Каждый — чья-то история, чей-то сигнал в будущее. Время — единственная абсолютно синхронная точка отсчёта на планете. Нет часового пояса, который обошёл бы полночь. В этой универсальности — сила. Я здесь не ради охвата. Я здесь ради точности своего голоса.`,
    `Прямо сейчас на планете идёт непрерывный эфир, который никто не контролирует централизованно. Граждане TimeNation занимают свои минуты — и каждая несёт реальную мысль реального человека. Не алгоритм, не бюджет. Просто голос, синхронизированный с глобальным временем. Именно так должно работать вещание будущего.`,
  ];

  const closings = [
    `Таймлайн принял мой сигнал. Координата запечатана.`,
    `Сеть слышит меня. Момент зафиксирован навсегда.`,
    `Минута выкуплена. Мой голос — часть глобального эфира.`,
  ];

  return `${openings[idx]}\n\n${bodies[idx]}\n\n${closings[idx]}`;
}

function hashStr(s: string): number {
  let h = 0;
  for (const c of s) { h = (Math.imul(31, h) + c.charCodeAt(0)) | 0; }
  return h;
}

// ── Google Cloud TTS (Service Account — Wavenet quality) ──────────────────────

function getGoogleTTSAuth() {
  const raw = process.env["GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON"];
  if (!raw) return null;
  try {
    const credentials = JSON.parse(raw);
    return new google.auth.GoogleAuth({
      credentials,
      scopes: ["https://www.googleapis.com/auth/cloud-platform"],
    });
  } catch (err) {
    logger.error({ err }, "Failed to parse GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON for TTS");
    return null;
  }
}

async function googleCloudTTS(text: string): Promise<Buffer | null> {
  const auth = getGoogleTTSAuth();
  if (!auth) return null;
  try {
    const accessToken = await auth.getAccessToken();
    if (!accessToken) return null;
    const response = await fetch(
      "https://texttospeech.googleapis.com/v1/text:synthesize",
      {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}` },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          input: { text: text.slice(0, 5000) },
          voice: { languageCode: GOOGLE_TTS_LANG, name: GOOGLE_TTS_VOICE },
          audioConfig: { audioEncoding: "MP3", speakingRate: 1.0, pitch: 0 },
        }),
      }
    );
    if (!response.ok) {
      const body = await response.text();
      logger.warn({ status: response.status, body }, "Google Cloud TTS error");
      return null;
    }
    const data = (await response.json()) as { audioContent?: string };
    if (!data.audioContent) return null;
    return Buffer.from(data.audioContent, "base64");
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err }, "Google Cloud TTS failed");
    return null;
  }
}

// ── Google Translate TTS fallback (no API key, chunked for >200 chars) ────────

/** Split text into sentence-aware chunks ≤200 chars */
function splitIntoChunks(text: string, maxLen = 180): string[] {
  const chunks: string[] = [];
  // Split at sentence boundaries first
  const sentences = text.split(/(?<=[.!?])\s+/);
  let cur = "";
  for (const sent of sentences) {
    if ((cur + " " + sent).trim().length <= maxLen) {
      cur = (cur + " " + sent).trim();
    } else {
      if (cur) chunks.push(cur);
      // If single sentence is too long, split by comma/clause
      if (sent.length > maxLen) {
        const parts = sent.split(/,\s*/);
        let sub = "";
        for (const p of parts) {
          if ((sub + ", " + p).trim().length <= maxLen) {
            sub = sub ? sub + ", " + p : p;
          } else {
            if (sub) chunks.push(sub);
            sub = p;
          }
        }
        if (sub) cur = sub;
        else cur = "";
      } else {
        cur = sent;
      }
    }
  }
  if (cur) chunks.push(cur);
  return chunks.filter(Boolean);
}

async function fetchChunkTTS(chunk: string): Promise<Buffer | null> {
  const url = `https://translate.google.com/translate_tts?ie=UTF-8&tl=ru&client=tw-ob&q=${encodeURIComponent(chunk)}`;
  try {
    const res = await fetch(url, {
      headers: { "User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (compatible; TimeNation/1.0)" },
    });
    if (!res.ok) { logger.warn({ status: res.status, chunk: chunk.slice(0, 30) }, "gTTS chunk failed"); return null; }
    return Buffer.from(await res.arrayBuffer());
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err }, "gTTS chunk fetch error");
    return null;
  }
}

async function googleTranslateTTS(text: string): Promise<Buffer | null> {
  const chunks = splitIntoChunks(text);
  if (chunks.length === 0) return null;
  logger.info({ chunks: chunks.length }, "gTTS: fetching chunks");

  const buffers = await Promise.all(chunks.map(fetchChunkTTS));
  const valid = buffers.filter((b): b is Buffer => b !== null);
  if (valid.length === 0) return null;
  if (valid.length === 1) return valid[0]!;

  // Concatenate MP3 chunks via FFmpeg concat filter
  const tmpDir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "gtts-"));
  try {
    const tmpFiles = valid.map((buf, i) => {
      const f = path.join(tmpDir, `chunk_${i}.mp3`);
      fs.writeFileSync(f, buf);
      return f;
    });
    const listFile = path.join(tmpDir, "list.txt");
    fs.writeFileSync(listFile, tmpFiles.map(f => `file '${f}'`).join("\n"));
    const outFile = path.join(tmpDir, "combined.mp3");

    await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
      const ff = spawn("ffmpeg", ["-y", "-f", "concat", "-safe", "0", "-i", listFile, "-c", "copy", outFile]);
      ff.on("close", code => code === 0 ? resolve() : reject(new Error(`ffmpeg concat exit ${code}`)));
    });

    return fs.readFileSync(outFile);
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err }, "gTTS: ffmpeg concat failed, returning first chunk");
    return valid[0] ?? null;
  } finally {
    fs.rmSync(tmpDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
}

// ── Main TTS entry point (Cloud TTS → gTTS fallback) ──────────────────────────

export async function generateTTSBuffer(text: string): Promise<Buffer | null> {
  // Try Google Cloud TTS first (Wavenet, highest quality)
  const cloudBuf = await googleCloudTTS(text);
  if (cloudBuf) {
    logger.info("TTS: using Google Cloud TTS (Wavenet)");
    return cloudBuf;
  }
  // Fallback: Google Translate TTS (no API key required)
  logger.info("TTS: Cloud TTS unavailable, falling back to Google Translate TTS");
  return googleTranslateTTS(text);
}

// ── Text helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function wrapLine(line: string, maxLen = 52): string[] {
  const words = line.split(/\s+/);
  const result: string[] = [];
  let cur = "";
  for (const w of words) {
    if (cur.length + w.length + 1 > maxLen) {
      if (cur) result.push(cur);
      cur = w;
    } else {
      cur = cur ? `${cur} ${w}` : w;
    }
  }
  if (cur) result.push(cur);
  return result;
}

function splitSegments(text: string, count: number): string[] {
  if (!text.trim()) return Array(count).fill("TimeNation TV");
  const sentences = text.split(/(?<=[.!?»])\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  const perGroup = Math.ceil(sentences.length / count);
  const segments: string[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < sentences.length; i += perGroup) {
    segments.push(sentences.slice(i, i + perGroup).join(" ").trim());
  }
  // Pad if fewer segments than expected
  while (segments.length < count) segments.push(segments.at(-1) ?? "");
  return segments.slice(0, count);
}

/** Escape text for FFmpeg drawtext filter value */
function esc(s: string): string {
  return s
    .replace(/\\/g, "\\\\")
    .replace(/'/g, "\u2019")   // curly apostrophe — avoids shell quoting issues
    .replace(/:/g, "\\:")
    .replace(/,/g, "\\,")
    .replace(/\[/g, "\\[")
    .replace(/\]/g, "\\]");
}

// ── Visual style themes (Shariah anti-Gharar: user pre-selects before payment) ─
export const VISUAL_STYLE_THEMES = {
  space:     { bg: "0x0D1B2A", title: "0x6B7E93", text: "0xC9A84C", accent: "0x4A90D9" },
  cyberpunk: { bg: "0x0A0014", title: "0xFF00FF", text: "0x00FFFF", accent: "0xFF6B00" },
  abstract:  { bg: "0x1A0A2E", title: "0x9B59B6", text: "0xE8D5B7", accent: "0xF39C12" },
  nature:    { bg: "0x0F1B0F", title: "0x4CAF50", text: "0xA8D8A8", accent: "0x8BC34A" },
  minimal:   { bg: "0x111111", title: "0x666666", text: "0xEEEEEE", accent: "0xFFFFFF" },
} as const;
export type VisualStyle = keyof typeof VISUAL_STYLE_THEMES;

// ── FFmpeg LIGHT render ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export async function renderLightVideo(
  audioBuffer: Buffer | null,
  manifestoText: string,
  slotId: number,
  visualStyle?: string | null,
  onProgress?: ProgressReporter
): Promise<string> {
  const tmpDir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), `tn-tv-${slotId}-`));
  const outputPath = path.join(MEDIA_DIR, `slot_${slotId}.mp4`);

  try {
    const segCount = Math.ceil(VIDEO_DURATION_SEC / SEGMENT_DURATION_SEC);
    const segments = splitSegments(manifestoText, segCount);

    // Apply visual style theme (Shariah anti-Gharar: user chose this before render)
    const style = visualStyle && visualStyle in VISUAL_STYLE_THEMES
      ? VISUAL_STYLE_THEMES[visualStyle as VisualStyle]
      : VISUAL_STYLE_THEMES.space;

    // Build drawtext filters: title always visible + rotating text segments
    const titleFilter =
      `drawtext=text='TIMENATION TV':fontsize=18:fontcolor=${style.title}:` +
      `x=(w-text_w)/2:y=48:alpha=0.8`;

    const slotFilter =
      `drawtext=text='SLOT ${slotId}':fontsize=14:fontcolor=${style.accent}@0.6:` +
      `x=(w-text_w)/2:y=78`;

    const segFilters: string[] = [];
    segments.forEach((seg, i) => {
      const t0 = i * SEGMENT_DURATION_SEC;
      const t1 = t0 + SEGMENT_DURATION_SEC;
      const lines = wrapLine(seg, 52).slice(0, 4);
      const totalH = lines.length * 42;
      const baseY = Math.round((720 - totalH) / 2);
      lines.forEach((line, li) => {
        segFilters.push(
          `drawtext=text='${esc(line)}':fontsize=28:fontcolor=${style.text}:` +
          `x=(w-text_w)/2:y=${baseY + li * 42}:` +
          `enable='between(t\\,${t0}\\,${t1})'`
        );
      });
    });

    // EU AI Act / China CAC: permanent AI watermark, bottom-right corner
    const watermarkFilter =
      `drawtext=text='TimeNation AI':fontsize=14:fontcolor=white@0.75:` +
      `x=w-tw-10:y=h-th-10:shadowcolor=black@0.5:shadowx=1:shadowy=1`;

    const vf = [titleFilter, slotFilter, ...segFilters, watermarkFilter].join(",");

    // Cryptographic metadata injection (EU AI Act / CAC Article 25)
    const metadataArgs = [
      "-metadata", `title=TimeNation-TV — Slot ${slotId}`,
      "-metadata", "comment=Author: TimeNation AI, Type: Synthesized Media",
      "-metadata", "artist=TimeNation",
      "-metadata", `description=AI Generated / Сгенерировано ИИ / Generated via Artificial Intelligence technologies. Visual style: ${visualStyle ?? "space"}.`,
    ];

    await onProgress?.("encoding");
    if (audioBuffer) {
      // ── 2-pass audio/video sync (as per Synchronized AI Capsule spec) ──────
      // Pass 1: generate visual-only MP4 with exactly VIDEO_DURATION_SEC duration
      const audioPath = path.join(tmpDir, "audio.mp3");
      const visualPath = path.join(tmpDir, "visual_only.mp4");
      fs.writeFileSync(audioPath, audioBuffer);

      await runFFmpeg([
        "-f", "lavfi", "-i", `color=c=${style.bg}:s=1280x720:r=25`,
        "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "anullsrc=r=44100:cl=stereo",
        "-vf", vf,
        "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "ultrafast", "-crf", "30",
        "-c:a", "aac",
        "-t", String(VIDEO_DURATION_SEC),
        "-y", visualPath,
      ]);

      // Pass 2: merge ElevenLabs audio into visual — copy video stream (no re-encode)
      await runFFmpeg([
        "-i", visualPath,
        "-i", audioPath,
        "-c:v", "copy",
        "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "128k",
        "-map", "0:v:0",
        "-map", "1:a:0",
        "-shortest",
        ...metadataArgs,
        "-y", outputPath,
      ]);
    } else {
      // No audio: single pass with silent track
      await runFFmpeg([
        "-f", "lavfi", "-i", `color=c=${style.bg}:s=1280x720:r=25`,
        "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "anullsrc=r=44100:cl=stereo",
        "-vf", vf,
        "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "ultrafast", "-crf", "30",
        "-c:a", "aac",
        "-t", String(VIDEO_DURATION_SEC),
        ...metadataArgs,
        "-y", outputPath,
      ]);
    }
    logger.info({ slotId, hasAudio: !!audioBuffer }, "LIGHT render complete");
    return `/api/tv/media/slot_${slotId}.mp4`;
  } finally {
    fs.rmSync(tmpDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
}

function runFFmpeg(args: string[]): Promise<void> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const proc = spawn("ffmpeg", args, { stdio: ["ignore", "ignore", "pipe"] });
    let stderr = "";
    proc.stderr?.on("data", (d: Buffer) => { stderr += d.toString(); });
    proc.on("close", (code) => {
      if (code === 0) resolve();
      else reject(new Error(`FFmpeg exited ${code}: ${stderr.slice(-1000)}`));
    });
    proc.on("error", (err) => reject(err));
  });
}

// ── Progress reporter ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
type ProgressReporter = (stage: string) => Promise<void>;

async function setRenderProgress(slotId: number, stage: string): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await db
      .update(tvCapsulesTable)
      .set({ renderProgress: stage, updatedAt: new Date() })
      .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId));
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn({ err, slotId, stage }, "Failed to update renderProgress");
  }
}

// ── AI Background Render ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Renders a video using an AI-generated background (HuggingFace or Pollinations),
// overlaying the text manifesto and TTS audio on top.
// Falls back to solid-color renderLightVideo if AI video unavailable.
async function renderWithAIBackground(
  audioBuffer: Buffer | null,
  manifestoText: string,
  slotId: number,
  visualStyle?: string | null,
  seedText?: string | null,
  citizenId?: number | null,
  onProgress?: ProgressReporter,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
  tier: RenderTier = "citizen"
): Promise<string> {
  const tmpDir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), `tn-ai-${slotId}-`));
  const outputPath = path.join(MEDIA_DIR, `slot_${slotId}.mp4`);

  try {
    // Step 1: Generate cinematic prompt via Gemini
    const style = (visualStyle && visualStyle in VISUAL_STYLE_THEMES
      ? visualStyle
      : "space") as CinematicVisualStyle;

    const cinematicPrompt = await generateCinematicPromptTiered(
      seedText ?? manifestoText.slice(0, 400),
      style,
      tier
    );

    // Step 1b: Load citizen name and rank for overlay
    let citizenName = "Гражданин TimeNation";
    let citizenRank = "Гражданин";
    if (citizenId) {
      try {
        const [citizen] = await db
          .select({ name: citizensTable.name, rank: citizensTable.rank })
          .from(citizensTable)
          .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
          .limit(1);
        if (citizen) {
          citizenName = citizen.name ?? citizenName;
          citizenRank = citizen.rank ?? citizenRank;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        logger.warn({ err, citizenId }, "Could not load citizen data for overlay");
      }
    }

    // Step 2: Fetch AI background video (HuggingFace → Pollinations → null)
    await onProgress?.("fetching_video");
    const { videoPath: aiVideoPath } = await generateAIBackground(
      cinematicPrompt,
      tmpDir,
      signal
    );

    if (!aiVideoPath) {
      // Total failure — fall back to solid-color light render
      logger.info({ slotId, style }, "AI background unavailable — falling back to solid-color render");
      return renderLightVideo(audioBuffer, manifestoText, slotId, visualStyle);
    }

    // Step 3: Build overlay filters (manifesto text + citizen rank/name)
    const theme = VISUAL_STYLE_THEMES[style];
    const segCount = Math.ceil(VIDEO_DURATION_SEC / SEGMENT_DURATION_SEC);
    const segments = splitSegments(manifestoText, segCount);

    const titleFilter =
      `drawtext=text='TIMENATION TV':fontsize=18:fontcolor=${theme.title}:` +
      `x=(w-text_w)/2:y=48:alpha=0.8`;

    const slotFilter =
      `drawtext=text='SLOT ${slotId}':fontsize=14:fontcolor=${theme.accent}@0.6:` +
      `x=(w-text_w)/2:y=78`;

    // Citizen rank (bottom-left, always visible)
    const rankFilter =
      `drawtext=text='${esc(citizenRank)}':fontsize=15:fontcolor=${theme.accent}@0.9:` +
      `x=12:y=h-th-34:shadowcolor=black@0.6:shadowx=1:shadowy=1`;

    // Citizen name (bottom-left below rank, always visible)
    const nameFilter =
      `drawtext=text='${esc(citizenName)}':fontsize=17:fontcolor=${theme.text}:` +
      `x=12:y=h-th-12:shadowcolor=black@0.6:shadowx=1:shadowy=1`;

    const segFilters: string[] = [];
    segments.forEach((seg, i) => {
      const t0 = i * SEGMENT_DURATION_SEC;
      const t1 = t0 + SEGMENT_DURATION_SEC;
      const lines = wrapLine(seg, 52).slice(0, 4);
      const totalH = lines.length * 42;
      const baseY = Math.round((720 - totalH) / 2);
      lines.forEach((line, li) => {
        segFilters.push(
          `drawtext=text='${esc(line)}':fontsize=28:fontcolor=${theme.text}:` +
          `x=(w-text_w)/2:y=${baseY + li * 42}:` +
          `enable='between(t\\,${t0}\\,${t1})'`
        );
      });
    });

    const watermarkFilter =
      `drawtext=text='TimeNation AI':fontsize=14:fontcolor=white@0.75:` +
      `x=w-tw-10:y=h-th-10:shadowcolor=black@0.5:shadowx=1:shadowy=1`;

    // Semi-transparent dark overlay to ensure text readability over any background
    const darkenFilter = `colorchannelmixer=aa=0.55`;

    const vf = [
      // Scale AI video to 1280×720, then darken slightly for readability
      `scale=1280:720:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease`,
      `pad=1280:720:(ow-iw)/2:(oh-ih)/2`,
      `setsar=1`,
      darkenFilter,
      titleFilter,
      slotFilter,
      rankFilter,
      nameFilter,
      ...segFilters,
      watermarkFilter,
    ].join(",");

    const metadataArgs = [
      "-metadata", `title=TimeNation-TV — Slot ${slotId}`,
      "-metadata", "comment=Author: TimeNation AI, Type: Synthesized Media",
      "-metadata", "artist=TimeNation",
      "-metadata", `description=AI Generated / Сгенерировано ИИ / AI cinematic background. Visual style: ${style}. Prompt: ${cinematicPrompt.slice(0, 120)}.`,
    ];

    await onProgress?.("encoding");
    if (audioBuffer) {
      const audioPath = path.join(tmpDir, "audio.mp3");
      fs.writeFileSync(audioPath, audioBuffer);

      await runFFmpeg([
        "-stream_loop", "-1", "-i", aiVideoPath,
        "-i", audioPath,
        "-vf", vf,
        "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "ultrafast", "-crf", "28",
        "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "128k",
        "-map", "0:v:0",
        "-map", "1:a:0",
        "-t", String(VIDEO_DURATION_SEC),
        "-shortest",
        ...metadataArgs,
        "-y", outputPath,
      ]);
    } else {
      await runFFmpeg([
        "-stream_loop", "-1", "-i", aiVideoPath,
        "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "anullsrc=r=44100:cl=stereo",
        "-vf", vf,
        "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "ultrafast", "-crf", "28",
        "-c:a", "aac",
        "-map", "0:v:0",
        "-map", "1:a:0",
        "-t", String(VIDEO_DURATION_SEC),
        ...metadataArgs,
        "-y", outputPath,
      ]);
    }

    logger.info({ slotId, style, cinematicPrompt: cinematicPrompt.slice(0, 80) }, "AI background render complete");
    return `/api/tv/media/slot_${slotId}.mp4`;
  } catch (err) {
    logger.error({ err, slotId }, "renderWithAIBackground failed — falling back to solid-color");
    return renderLightVideo(audioBuffer, manifestoText, slotId, visualStyle, onProgress);
  } finally {
    fs.rmSync(tmpDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
}

// ── Prime Time Render (:00 — multi-scene 3-part montage with xfade) ─────────────
async function renderPrimeVideo(
  audioBuffer: Buffer | null,
  manifestoText: string,
  slotId: number,
  visualStyle?: string | null,
  seedText?: string | null,
  citizenId?: number | null,
  onProgress?: ProgressReporter,
  signal?: AbortSignal
): Promise<string> {
  const tmpDir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), `tn-prime-${slotId}-`));
  const outputPath = path.join(MEDIA_DIR, `slot_${slotId}.mp4`);

  try {
    const style = (visualStyle && visualStyle in VISUAL_STYLE_THEMES
      ? visualStyle
      : "space") as CinematicVisualStyle;
    const theme = VISUAL_STYLE_THEMES[style];

    // Load citizen identity for overlay
    let citizenName = "Гражданин TimeNation";
    let citizenRank = "Гражданин";
    if (citizenId) {
      try {
        const [citizen] = await db
          .select({ name: citizensTable.name, rank: citizensTable.rank })
          .from(citizensTable)
          .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
          .limit(1);
        if (citizen) {
          citizenName = citizen.name ?? citizenName;
          citizenRank = citizen.rank ?? citizenRank;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        logger.warn({ err, citizenId }, "Could not load citizen data for PRIME overlay");
      }
    }

    // Step 1 — Generate 3 unique scene prompts via Gemini
    await onProgress?.("generating_scenes");
    const scenePrompts = await generateMultiScenePrompts(
      seedText ?? manifestoText.slice(0, 400),
      manifestoText,
      style
    );
    logger.info(
      { slotId, s1: scenePrompts.scene1.slice(0, 50) },
      "PRIME: 3 scene prompts generated"
    );

    // Step 2 — AI background per scene (sequential, per-scene solid-color fallback)
    await onProgress?.("fetching_scenes");
    const sceneVideoFiles: string[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      const prompt = [scenePrompts.scene1, scenePrompts.scene2, scenePrompts.scene3][i]!;
      const sceneDir = path.join(tmpDir, `scene${i + 1}`);
      fs.mkdirSync(sceneDir, { recursive: true });

      try {
        const { videoPath } = await generateAIBackground(prompt, sceneDir, signal);
        if (!videoPath) throw new Error("generateAIBackground returned null");
        sceneVideoFiles.push(videoPath);
        logger.info({ slotId, scene: i + 1 }, `PRIME scene ${i + 1} AI background ready`);
      } catch (err) {
        // Per-scene fallback: solid-color 22s clip
        const fallbackPath = path.join(sceneDir, "fallback.mp4");
        await runFFmpeg([
          "-f", "lavfi", "-i", `color=c=${theme.bg}:s=1280x720:r=25`,
          "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "anullsrc=r=44100:cl=stereo",
          "-t", "22", "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "ultrafast", "-crf", "30",
          "-c:a", "aac", "-y", fallbackPath,
        ]);
        sceneVideoFiles.push(fallbackPath);
        logger.warn({ slotId, scene: i + 1, err }, `PRIME scene ${i + 1} AI failed — solid-color fallback used`);
      }
    }

    // Step 3 — Xfade montage: 3×22s clips → ~62s composite, trimmed to 60s
    //   offset=18: scene1 full 18s → 2s dissolve into scene2 (starts at t=18, ends t=20)
    //   offset=36: scene2 full from t=20-36 → 2s dissolve into scene3 (ends t=38)
    //   scene3 visible from t=38 to t=60
    await onProgress?.("compositing");
    const compositePath = path.join(tmpDir, "composite.mp4");

    await runFFmpeg([
      "-stream_loop", "-1", "-t", "22", "-i", sceneVideoFiles[0]!,
      "-stream_loop", "-1", "-t", "22", "-i", sceneVideoFiles[1]!,
      "-stream_loop", "-1", "-t", "22", "-i", sceneVideoFiles[2]!,
      "-filter_complex",
        "[0:v]scale=1280:720:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease," +
        "pad=1280:720:(ow-iw)/2:(oh-ih)/2,setsar=1[sv0];" +
        "[1:v]scale=1280:720:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease," +
        "pad=1280:720:(ow-iw)/2:(oh-ih)/2,setsar=1[sv1];" +
        "[2:v]scale=1280:720:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease," +
        "pad=1280:720:(ow-iw)/2:(oh-ih)/2,setsar=1[sv2];" +
        "[sv0][sv1]xfade=transition=dissolve:duration=2:offset=18[v01];" +
        "[v01][sv2]xfade=transition=dissolve:duration=2:offset=36[vout]",
      "-map", "[vout]",
      "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "ultrafast", "-crf", "26",
      "-t", "60", "-y", compositePath,
    ]);
    logger.info({ slotId }, "PRIME xfade composite assembled");

    // Step 4 — Text overlay + audio over the composite
    await onProgress?.("encoding");

    // 3 text segments: each shown only during its scene's "clear" window
    const segments = splitSegments(manifestoText, 3);
    const sceneTimes = [
      { t0: 0,  t1: 18 },  // scene 1: fully visible 0-18s
      { t0: 20, t1: 38 },  // scene 2: fully visible 20-38s
      { t0: 40, t1: 60 },  // scene 3: fully visible 40-60s
    ] as const;

    const titleFilter =
      `drawtext=text='TIMENATION PRIME':fontsize=20:fontcolor=${theme.title}:` +
      `x=(w-text_w)/2:y=44:alpha=0.9`;
    const slotFilter =
      `drawtext=text='SLOT ${slotId} — ПРАЙМ ТАЙМ':fontsize=14:fontcolor=${theme.accent}@0.85:` +
      `x=(w-text_w)/2:y=72`;
    const rankFilter =
      `drawtext=text='${esc(citizenRank)}':fontsize=15:fontcolor=${theme.accent}@0.9:` +
      `x=12:y=h-th-34:shadowcolor=black@0.6:shadowx=1:shadowy=1`;
    const nameFilter =
      `drawtext=text='${esc(citizenName)}':fontsize=17:fontcolor=${theme.text}:` +
      `x=12:y=h-th-12:shadowcolor=black@0.6:shadowx=1:shadowy=1`;

    const segFilters: string[] = [];
    segments.forEach((seg, i) => {
      const { t0, t1 } = sceneTimes[i]!;
      const lines = wrapLine(seg, 52).slice(0, 4);
      const totalH = lines.length * 42;
      const baseY = Math.round((720 - totalH) / 2);
      lines.forEach((line, li) => {
        segFilters.push(
          `drawtext=text='${esc(line)}':fontsize=28:fontcolor=${theme.text}:` +
          `x=(w-text_w)/2:y=${baseY + li * 42}:` +
          `enable='between(t\\,${t0}\\,${t1})'`
        );
      });
    });

    const watermarkFilter =
      `drawtext=text='TimeNation AI — PRIME':fontsize=14:fontcolor=white@0.75:` +
      `x=w-tw-10:y=h-th-10:shadowcolor=black@0.5:shadowx=1:shadowy=1`;
    const darkenFilter = `colorchannelmixer=aa=0.55`;

    const vf = [darkenFilter, titleFilter, slotFilter, rankFilter, nameFilter, ...segFilters, watermarkFilter].join(",");

    const metadataArgs = [
      "-metadata", `title=TimeNation-TV — Slot ${slotId} — PRIME TIME`,
      "-metadata", "comment=Author: TimeNation AI, Type: Synthesized Media — 3-Scene Prime Montage",
      "-metadata", "artist=TimeNation",
      "-metadata", `description=AI Prime Montage / 3 scenes: ${scenePrompts.scene1.slice(0, 40)} | ${scenePrompts.scene2.slice(0, 40)} | ${scenePrompts.scene3.slice(0, 40)}.`,
    ];

    if (audioBuffer) {
      const audioPath = path.join(tmpDir, "audio.mp3");
      fs.writeFileSync(audioPath, audioBuffer);
      await runFFmpeg([
        "-i", compositePath,
        "-i", audioPath,
        "-vf", vf,
        "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "fast", "-crf", "24",
        "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "192k",
        "-map", "0:v:0", "-map", "1:a:0",
        "-t", String(VIDEO_DURATION_SEC), "-shortest",
        ...metadataArgs, "-y", outputPath,
      ]);
    } else {
      await runFFmpeg([
        "-i", compositePath,
        "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "anullsrc=r=44100:cl=stereo",
        "-vf", vf,
        "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "fast", "-crf", "24",
        "-c:a", "aac",
        "-map", "0:v:0", "-map", "1:a:0",
        "-t", String(VIDEO_DURATION_SEC),
        ...metadataArgs, "-y", outputPath,
      ]);
    }

    logger.info(
      { slotId, style, s1: scenePrompts.scene1.slice(0, 40), s2: scenePrompts.scene2.slice(0, 40), s3: scenePrompts.scene3.slice(0, 40) },
      "PRIME TIME 3-scene montage render complete"
    );
    return `/api/tv/media/slot_${slotId}.mp4`;

  } catch (err) {
    logger.error({ err, slotId }, "renderPrimeVideo failed — falling back to apex AI background");
    return renderWithAIBackground(
      audioBuffer, manifestoText, slotId, visualStyle, seedText, citizenId, onProgress, signal, "apex"
    );
  } finally {
    fs.rmSync(tmpDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
}

// ── Main Orchestrator ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export async function renderCapsule(slotId: number, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<{
  manifestoText: string;
  mediaUrl: string | null;
  catcastUrl: string | null;
  status: string;
}> {
  const [capsule] = await db
    .select()
    .from(tvCapsulesTable)
    .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId))
    .limit(1);

  if (!capsule) throw new Error(`No capsule found for slotId=${slotId}`);

  // Mark as processing
  const reportProgress: ProgressReporter = (stage) => setRenderProgress(slotId, stage);

  await db
    .update(tvCapsulesTable)
    .set({ status: "pending_render", renderProgress: "generating_prompt", updatedAt: new Date() })
    .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId));

  // 1. Load slot for date/minuteIndex (needed for Gemini prompt context)
  const [slot] = await db
    .select({ date: tvSlotsTable.date, minuteIndex: tvSlotsTable.minuteIndex })
    .from(tvSlotsTable)
    .where(eq(tvSlotsTable.id, slotId))
    .limit(1);

  // 2. Determine manifesto text
  let manifestoText: string;
  if (capsule.contentState === "empty") {
    manifestoText = "TimeNation — пространство тишины. Момент принадлежит тебе.";
  } else if (capsule.mode === "b") {
    // Mode B: raw seed, no LLM expansion
    manifestoText = capsule.seedText ?? "";
  } else {
    // Mode A: Gemini AI packaging → fallback to template if key missing or error
    if (capsule.seedText && slot) {
      const geminiResult = await generateManifesto(
        capsule.seedText,
        slot.date,
        slot.minuteIndex
      );
      manifestoText = geminiResult ?? expandSeedToManifesto(capsule.seedText);
      if (!geminiResult) {
        logger.info({ slotId }, "Gemini unavailable — used template manifesto fallback");
      }
    } else {
      manifestoText = capsule.seedText
        ? expandSeedToManifesto(capsule.seedText)
        : "TimeNation — суверенное государство времени.";
    }
  }

  // 2. Three-tier render routing by slot type ──────────────────────────────
  //    apex (:00)    → renderPrimeVideo   (3-scene Gemini + FFmpeg xfade montage)
  //    half (:30)    → renderWithAIBackground tier=half  (premium quality prompt)
  //    quarter (:15/:45) → renderWithAIBackground tier=quarter (high quality prompt)
  //    citizen       → renderWithAIBackground tier=citizen (standard quality)
  const slotType = slot ? getSlotType(slot.minuteIndex) : "citizen";
  const tier: RenderTier = slotType;

  let mediaUrl: string | null = null;
  let finalStatus = "draft";

  // Generate TTS audio once (shared by all paths)
  await reportProgress("generating_tts");
  const audioBuffer = manifestoText ? await generateTTSBuffer(manifestoText) : null;

  if (slotType === "apex") {
    // PRIME TIME (:00) — 3-scene multi-montage (Gemini splits text → 3 AI frames → xfade)
    try {
      mediaUrl = await renderPrimeVideo(
        audioBuffer, manifestoText, slotId,
        capsule.visualStyle, capsule.seedText, capsule.citizenId,
        reportProgress, signal
      );
      finalStatus = "rendered";
      logger.info({ slotId }, "PRIME TIME render complete");
    } catch (err) {
      logger.error({ err, slotId }, "renderPrimeVideo failed — falling back to apex AI background");
      try {
        mediaUrl = await renderWithAIBackground(
          audioBuffer, manifestoText, slotId,
          capsule.visualStyle, capsule.seedText, capsule.citizenId,
          reportProgress, signal, "apex"
        );
        finalStatus = "rendered";
      } catch (bgErr) {
        logger.error({ bgErr, slotId }, "APEX AI background also failed — solid-color fallback");
        try {
          mediaUrl = await renderLightVideo(audioBuffer, manifestoText, slotId, capsule.visualStyle, reportProgress);
          finalStatus = "rendered";
        } catch (lightErr) {
          logger.error({ lightErr, slotId }, "renderLightVideo fallback also failed");
          finalStatus = "draft";
        }
      }
    }
  } else {
    // HALF / QUARTER / CITIZEN — AI background with tier-specific prompt quality
    try {
      mediaUrl = await renderWithAIBackground(
        audioBuffer, manifestoText, slotId,
        capsule.visualStyle, capsule.seedText, capsule.citizenId,
        reportProgress, signal, tier
      );
      finalStatus = "rendered";
      logger.info({ slotId, tier }, `${tier.toUpperCase()} slot rendered with AI background`);
    } catch (err) {
      logger.error({ err, slotId, tier }, `${tier.toUpperCase()} renderWithAIBackground failed — solid-color fallback`);
      try {
        mediaUrl = await renderLightVideo(audioBuffer, manifestoText, slotId, capsule.visualStyle, reportProgress);
        finalStatus = "rendered";
      } catch (lightErr) {
        logger.error({ lightErr, slotId }, "renderLightVideo also failed");
        finalStatus = "draft";
      }
    }
  }

  // 4. Build storage record and persist
  const localPath = path.join(MEDIA_DIR, `slot_${slotId}.mp4`);
  let sizeBytes: number | undefined;
  try {
    sizeBytes = fs.existsSync(localPath) ? fs.statSync(localPath).size : undefined;
  } catch { /* ignore */ }
  const storageRecord = buildStorageRecord(slotId, localPath, sizeBytes);
  // Use hot CDN URL as the canonical mediaUrl if available
  const canonicalMediaUrl = storageRecord.hotUrl ?? mediaUrl;

  await db
    .update(tvCapsulesTable)
    .set({
      manifestoText: manifestoText || null,
      mediaUrl: canonicalMediaUrl,
      status: finalStatus,
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    })
    .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId));

  // 5. Upload to Catcast.tv (async, non-blocking for caller)
  let catcastUrl: string | null = null;
  if (finalStatus === "rendered" && mediaUrl) {
    const outputPath = path.join(MEDIA_DIR, `slot_${slotId}.mp4`);
    const [citizen] = await db
      .select({ name: citizensTable.name })
      .from(citizensTable)
      .where(eq(citizensTable.id, capsule.citizenId))
      .limit(1);
    const citizenName = citizen?.name ?? "Гражданин TimeNation";

    const catcastResult = await uploadToCatcast(
      outputPath,
      slotId,
      citizenName,
      capsule.seedText ?? ""
    ).catch((err) => {
      logger.warn({ err, slotId }, "Catcast upload threw, skipping");
      return null;
    });

    if (catcastResult) {
      catcastUrl = catcastResult.videoUrl;
      await db
        .update(tvCapsulesTable)
        .set({
          catcastVideoId: catcastResult.videoId,
          catcastUrl: catcastResult.videoUrl,
          updatedAt: new Date(),
        })
        .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId));
      logger.info({ slotId, catcastUrl }, "Capsule published to Catcast");
    }
  }

  return { manifestoText, mediaUrl, catcastUrl, status: finalStatus };
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/services/renderQueue.ts ---
import { randomUUID } from "crypto";
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { tvSlotsTable, tvCapsulesTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { renderCapsule, renderLightVideo } from "./renderService";
import { sendTransactionNotification } from "./telegramService";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";

// ── Render Queue ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Limits concurrent AI renders so slow HuggingFace requests (up to 120s each)
// don't pile up and starve other API handlers.
//
// Design: simple in-process FIFO queue with a configurable concurrency cap.
// The DB capsule status (pending_render → rendered / draft) is the durable
// state; this queue is the execution gatekeeper only.

const MAX_CONCURRENT_RENDERS = parseInt(
  process.env["MAX_CONCURRENT_RENDERS"] ?? "2",
  10
);

/**
 * Per-job wall-clock timeout. If a render job exceeds this, it is abandoned
 * and an immediate solid-color fallback is produced so the queue keeps moving.
 * Default: 180 s. Override with RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_MS env var (generic fallback).
 */
const RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_MS = parseInt(
  process.env["RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_MS"] ?? "180000",
  10
);

/**
 * Timeout for light-path renders (citizen 1-minute slots).
 * Default: 120 s. Override with RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_LIGHT_MS env var.
 */
export const RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_LIGHT_MS = parseInt(
  process.env["RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_LIGHT_MS"] ?? "120000",
  10
);

/**
 * Timeout for heavy-path renders (Quarter / Half / Apex premium slots).
 * Default: 300 s. Override with RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_HEAVY_MS env var.
 */
export const RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_HEAVY_MS = parseInt(
  process.env["RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_HEAVY_MS"] ?? "300000",
  10
);

/** Sentinel error thrown by the per-job deadline race. */
class RenderTimeoutError extends Error {
  readonly elapsed: number;
  constructor(slotId: number, elapsed: number) {
    super(`Render timed out after ${elapsed}ms for slotId=${slotId}`);
    this.name = "RenderTimeoutError";
    this.elapsed = elapsed;
  }
}

interface RenderJob {
  jobId: string;
  slotId: number;
  citizenId: number;
  capsuleSeedText: string | null;
  /** Per-job timeout override in ms. Defaults to RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_MS when omitted. */
  timeoutMs?: number;
}

let activeCount = 0;
const queue: Array<() => void> = [];

/** Jobs indexed by jobId for status look-up. */
const jobStatus = new Map<
  string,
  { slotId: number; status: "queued" | "running" | "done" | "failed" }
>();

function drainQueue(): void {
  while (activeCount < MAX_CONCURRENT_RENDERS && queue.length > 0) {
    const next = queue.shift();
    next?.();
  }
}

/**
 * Enqueue a render job.
 * Returns a jobId immediately. The job will run when a concurrency slot opens.
 */
export function enqueueRender(job: RenderJob): string {
  const { jobId, slotId, citizenId, capsuleSeedText } = job;
  const effectiveTimeoutMs = job.timeoutMs ?? RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_MS;

  jobStatus.set(jobId, { slotId, status: "queued" });

  const run = () => {
    activeCount++;
    const entry = jobStatus.get(jobId);
    if (entry) entry.status = "running";

    const renderLog = logger.child({ slotId, citizenId, jobId });
    renderLog.info({ queueDepth: queue.length, active: activeCount, timeoutMs: effectiveTimeoutMs }, "Render job started");

    void (async () => {
      const startTime = Date.now();

      // AbortController shared with the render job — aborted when the deadline fires
      const abortController = new AbortController();

      // Build a deadline promise that rejects once effectiveTimeoutMs elapses
      let deadlineTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
      const deadline = new Promise<never>((_, reject) => {
        deadlineTimer = setTimeout(() => {
          const elapsed = Date.now() - startTime;
          abortController.abort();
          reject(new RenderTimeoutError(slotId, elapsed));
        }, effectiveTimeoutMs);
      });

      try {
        const result = await Promise.race([renderCapsule(slotId, abortController.signal), deadline]);

        clearTimeout(deadlineTimer);

        if (result.mediaUrl) {
          await db
            .update(tvSlotsTable)
            .set({ status: "broadcast" })
            .where(eq(tvSlotsTable.id, slotId));
          await db
            .update(tvCapsulesTable)
            .set({ status: "broadcast", renderProgress: "done", updatedAt: new Date() })
            .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId));
          renderLog.info("Capsule auto-published to broadcast");
        }

        const entry2 = jobStatus.get(jobId);
        if (entry2) entry2.status = "done";

        const tgMessage =
          `📡 *Капсула готова к эфиру!*\n\n` +
          `Слот #${slotId} отрендерен.\n` +
          (result.mediaUrl
            ? `🎬 Видео доступно в приложении TimeNation TV.`
            : `⚠️ Видео не сгенерировано.`);
        sendTransactionNotification(citizenId, tgMessage).catch((err) => {
          renderLog.warn({ err }, "Telegram notification failed after render");
        });
      } catch (err) {
        clearTimeout(deadlineTimer);

        if (err instanceof RenderTimeoutError) {
          // ── Timeout path: solid-color immediate fallback ──────────────────
          const elapsed = err.elapsed;
          renderLog.warn(
            { slotId, jobId, elapsed, timeoutMs: effectiveTimeoutMs },
            "Render job exceeded wall-clock timeout — falling back to solid-color render"
          );

          try {
            const [capsule] = await db
              .select({
                seedText: tvCapsulesTable.seedText,
                visualStyle: tvCapsulesTable.visualStyle,
              })
              .from(tvCapsulesTable)
              .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId))
              .limit(1);

            const manifestoText =
              capsule?.seedText ?? "TimeNation — суверенное государство времени.";

            const mediaUrl = await renderLightVideo(
              null,
              manifestoText,
              slotId,
              capsule?.visualStyle ?? null
            );

            await db
              .update(tvCapsulesTable)
              .set({ status: "rendered", renderProgress: "done", updatedAt: new Date() })
              .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId));
            await db
              .update(tvSlotsTable)
              .set({ status: "broadcast" })
              .where(eq(tvSlotsTable.id, slotId));

            renderLog.info(
              { slotId, jobId, elapsed, mediaUrl },
              "Solid-color fallback render complete after timeout"
            );

            const entry3 = jobStatus.get(jobId);
            if (entry3) entry3.status = "done";

            sendTransactionNotification(
              citizenId,
              `⏱ *Слот #${slotId} отрендерен* (упрощённый режим — AI-генерация превысила лимит времени)`
            ).catch((tgErr) => {
              renderLog.warn({ tgErr }, "Telegram notification failed after timeout fallback");
            });
          } catch (fallbackErr) {
            renderLog.error(
              { fallbackErr, slotId, jobId, elapsed },
              "Solid-color fallback render also failed after timeout"
            );

            const entry4 = jobStatus.get(jobId);
            if (entry4) entry4.status = "failed";

            await db
              .update(tvCapsulesTable)
              .set({ status: "draft", renderProgress: null, updatedAt: new Date() })
              .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId))
              .catch(() => {});
          }
        } else {
          // ── Normal error path ─────────────────────────────────────────────
          renderLog.error({ err }, "Render job failed");

          const entry5 = jobStatus.get(jobId);
          if (entry5) entry5.status = "failed";

          await db
            .update(tvCapsulesTable)
            .set({ status: "draft", renderProgress: null, updatedAt: new Date() })
            .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.slotId, slotId))
            .catch(() => {});
        }
      } finally {
        activeCount--;
        renderLog.info(
          { remaining: queue.length, active: activeCount },
          "Render job finished — draining queue"
        );
        drainQueue();

        // Evict completed job status after 1 hour to prevent unbounded growth
        setTimeout(() => jobStatus.delete(jobId), 60 * 60 * 1000);
      }
    })();
  };

  if (activeCount < MAX_CONCURRENT_RENDERS) {
    run();
  } else {
    logger.info(
      { slotId, jobId, queueDepth: queue.length, active: activeCount },
      "Render job queued — concurrency cap reached"
    );
    queue.push(run);
  }

  return jobId;
}

/** Queue depth and active count for health/debug endpoints. */
export function getRenderQueueStats(): {
  active: number;
  queued: number;
  maxConcurrent: number;
} {
  return { active: activeCount, queued: queue.length, maxConcurrent: MAX_CONCURRENT_RENDERS };
}

/** Look up a job's current status. Returns null if unknown/evicted. */
export function getRenderJobStatus(jobId: string): {
  slotId: number;
  status: "queued" | "running" | "done" | "failed";
} | null {
  return jobStatus.get(jobId) ?? null;
}

/**
 * On server startup, re-queue any capsules stuck in `pending_render`.
 * These are jobs that were lost when the server crashed or was restarted.
 * The tvCapsulesTable.status column is the durable source of truth.
 */
export async function rehydrateRenderQueue(): Promise<void> {
  const stuck = await db
    .select({
      slotId: tvCapsulesTable.slotId,
      citizenId: tvCapsulesTable.citizenId,
      seedText: tvCapsulesTable.seedText,
      renderPath: tvCapsulesTable.renderPath,
    })
    .from(tvCapsulesTable)
    .where(eq(tvCapsulesTable.status, "pending_render"));

  if (stuck.length === 0) {
    logger.info("Render queue rehydration: no stuck jobs found");
    return;
  }

  logger.info(
    { count: stuck.length },
    "Render queue rehydration: re-queuing capsules stuck in pending_render"
  );

  for (const capsule of stuck) {
    const jobId = randomUUID();
    const timeoutMs =
      capsule.renderPath === "heavy"
        ? RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_HEAVY_MS
        : RENDER_JOB_TIMEOUT_LIGHT_MS;
    enqueueRender({
      jobId,
      slotId: capsule.slotId,
      citizenId: capsule.citizenId,
      capsuleSeedText: capsule.seedText,
      timeoutMs,
    });
  }
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/services/geminiService.ts ---
import { GoogleGenerativeAI } from "@google/generative-ai";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";

// ── Gemini client (lazy-init, falls back gracefully if key missing) ───────────
let _genai: GoogleGenerativeAI | null = null;

function getClient(): GoogleGenerativeAI | null {
  if (_genai) return _genai;
  const key = process.env["GOOGLE_API_KEY"];
  if (!key) {
    logger.warn("GOOGLE_API_KEY not set — Gemini manifest generation disabled");
    return null;
  }
  _genai = new GoogleGenerativeAI(key);
  return _genai;
}

// ── System prompt for manifesto generation ────────────────────────────────────
// Designed for 60-second voiced broadcast (≈120–140 words at natural Russian speech pace)
const SYSTEM_PROMPT = `Ты — TimeNation Thought Expander, нейронный усилитель голоса гражданина цифрового государства.

ЦЕЛЬ: Взять короткую мысль гражданина и развернуть её в точный 60-секундный манифест о синхронизированном глобальном вещании и суверенитете времени — в точном голосе самого гражданина.

ПРАВИЛА:
- Длина: 120–140 слов. Это ровно 60 секунд звучания при естественном темпе речи.
- Язык: живой русский, первое лицо («я», «мы»). Звучит как живой человек — не как академический манифест.
- Стиль: личный, убеждённый, кинематографически точный. Без пафоса ради пафоса.
- Структура: вступление (2 предл.) → ядро мысли (3–4 предл.) → контекст TimeNation (2 предл.) → финал с зафиксированной координатой (1 предл.).
- Только связный текст, пригодный для озвучки. Никаких заголовков, списков, кавычек, скобок.
- Упомяни: 1440 минут в сутках, глобальное вещание как акт суверенитета.
- НЕ упоминай реальные деньги, реальную политику или конкретные страны.
- Завершить словом о зафиксированном моменте: "Координата запечатана", "Сеть слышит меня", "Таймлайн принял мой сигнал" или аналогичным.`;

// ── Retry with exponential backoff ───────────────────────────────────────────
async function withRetry<T>(
  fn: () => Promise<T>,
  retries = 3,
  baseDelayMs = 1000
): Promise<T> {
  let lastErr: unknown;
  for (let i = 0; i < retries; i++) {
    try {
      return await fn();
    } catch (err) {
      lastErr = err;
      const delay = baseDelayMs * 2 ** i;
      logger.warn({ err, attempt: i + 1, retries }, `Gemini retry in ${delay}ms`);
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, delay));
    }
  }
  throw lastErr;
}

// ── Generate manifesto via Gemini ─────────────────────────────────────────────
export async function generateManifesto(
  seedText: string,
  slotDate: string,
  minuteIndex: number
): Promise<string | null> {
  const client = getClient();
  if (!client) return null;

  const hour = String(Math.floor(minuteIndex / 60)).padStart(2, "0");
  const min = String(minuteIndex % 60).padStart(2, "0");

  const userPrompt =
    `Дата эфира: ${slotDate} ${hour}:${min} UTC\n` +
    `Зерно мысли гражданина: «${seedText.trim().slice(0, 400)}»\n\n` +
    `Напиши манифест-капсулу для этой временной координаты.`;

  try {
    const manifesto = await withRetry(async () => {
      const model = client.getGenerativeModel({
        model: "gemini-2.0-flash",
        systemInstruction: SYSTEM_PROMPT,
        generationConfig: {
          maxOutputTokens: 512,
          temperature: 0.88,
          topP: 0.93,
        },
      });

      const result = await model.generateContent(userPrompt);
      const text = result.response.text().trim();
      if (!text) throw new Error("Empty Gemini response");
      return text;
    });

    logger.info(
      { slotDate, minuteIndex, words: manifesto.split(/\s+/).length },
      "Gemini manifesto generated"
    );
    return manifesto;
  } catch (err) {
    logger.error({ err, seedText: seedText.slice(0, 80) }, "Gemini manifest generation failed");
    return null;
  }
}

// ── Check if Gemini is configured ─────────────────────────────────────────────
export function isGeminiConfigured(): boolean {
  return !!process.env["GOOGLE_API_KEY"];
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/services/encryptionService.ts ---
/**
 * AES-256-GCM symmetric encryption for PII fields (email, surname, givenNames).
 *
 * Key source (priority order):
 *   1. ENCRYPTION_KEY env var — 32-byte hex (64 hex chars) or base64 (44 chars)
 *   2. Derived from SESSION_SECRET via scrypt (development fallback, logs a warning)
 *
 * Format: `v1:<iv_hex>:<authTag_hex>:<ciphertext_hex>`
 * Prefix `v1:` lets future versions detect format changes.
 * Values that don't start with `v1:` are treated as plaintext (backwards-compat).
 */

import crypto from "crypto";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";

const ALGO = "aes-256-gcm";

function resolveKey(): Buffer {
  const raw = process.env["ENCRYPTION_KEY"];
  if (raw) {
    if (raw.length === 64) return Buffer.from(raw, "hex");
    if (raw.length === 44) return Buffer.from(raw, "base64");
    if (raw.length >= 32)  return Buffer.from(raw.slice(0, 32));
  }

  // Fallback: derive from SESSION_SECRET (acceptable in development)
  const secret = process.env["SESSION_SECRET"] ?? "timenation-dev-secret";
  const salt   = Buffer.from("timenation-pii-salt-v1");
  logger.warn("ENCRYPTION_KEY not set — deriving key from SESSION_SECRET (set ENCRYPTION_KEY in production)");
  return crypto.scryptSync(secret, salt, 32);
}

// Resolved once at module load
const KEY: Buffer = resolveKey();

// ── Public API ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** Encrypt plaintext → `v1:<iv>:<authTag>:<ciphertext>` (hex-encoded parts) */
export function encrypt(plaintext: string): string {
  const iv     = crypto.randomBytes(12);
  const cipher = crypto.createCipheriv(ALGO, KEY, iv);
  const ct     = Buffer.concat([cipher.update(plaintext, "utf8"), cipher.final()]);
  const tag    = cipher.getAuthTag();
  return `v1:${iv.toString("hex")}:${tag.toString("hex")}:${ct.toString("hex")}`;
}

/** Decrypt a `v1:…` ciphertext back to plaintext. Returns null on any error. */
export function decrypt(ciphertext: string): string | null {
  if (!ciphertext.startsWith("v1:")) return null;
  try {
    const parts = ciphertext.split(":");
    if (parts.length !== 4) return null;
    const [, ivHex, tagHex, ctHex] = parts as [string, string, string, string];
    const decipher = crypto.createDecipheriv(ALGO, KEY, Buffer.from(ivHex, "hex"));
    decipher.setAuthTag(Buffer.from(tagHex, "hex"));
    return decipher.update(Buffer.from(ctHex, "hex")).toString("utf8") + decipher.final("utf8");
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Safe decrypt: returns the original value if it is not encrypted (legacy rows)
 * or null/undefined as-is. Never throws.
 */
export function tryDecrypt(value: string | null | undefined): string | null | undefined {
  if (value == null) return value;
  if (!value.startsWith("v1:")) return value; // plaintext legacy row
  return decrypt(value) ?? value;             // return raw on failure (don't lose data)
}

/** Encrypt a nullable string — returns null unchanged */
export function encryptNullable(value: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  if (!value) return null;
  return encrypt(value);
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/services/telegramService.ts ---
import { Telegraf } from "telegraf";
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { citizensTable, transactionsTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { eq, and, ilike } from "drizzle-orm";
import { logger } from "../lib/logger";

let bot: Telegraf | null = null;

export function getBot(): Telegraf | null {
  return bot;
}

// ─── Legacy import data ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// amount кодирует: minutes * 100 + seconds (например, 261 = 2 мин 61 сек).
// fromName / toName сопоставляются с citizens.name в БД при импорте.
// sessionId = 0 — sentinel-маркер «импортированная запись».
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
interface LegacyTransaction {
  fromName: string;
  toName: string;
  amount: number; // minutes * 100 + seconds
  createdAt: string; // ISO 8601
}

const LEGACY_TRANSACTIONS: LegacyTransaction[] = [
  { fromName: "Gennadii Lerner", toName: "Olga Fedorova", amount: 261,  createdAt: "2026-05-27T07:31:00Z" },
  { fromName: "Gennadii Lerner", toName: "Olga Fedorova", amount: 299,  createdAt: "2026-05-27T09:05:00Z" },
  { fromName: "Gennadii Lerner", toName: "Olga Fedorova", amount: 262,  createdAt: "2026-05-27T09:42:00Z" },
  { fromName: "Gennadii Lerner", toName: "Gleb Lerner",   amount: 1160, createdAt: "2026-05-29T21:51:00Z" },
];

// ─── Admin guard ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Установи ADMIN_TELEGRAM_ID в Replit Secrets — числовой ID твоего аккаунта.
// Узнать свой ID можно у @userinfobot в Telegram.
const ADMIN_TELEGRAM_ID = process.env.ADMIN_TELEGRAM_ID ?? "";

function isAdmin(telegramId: string): boolean {
  return !!ADMIN_TELEGRAM_ID && telegramId === ADMIN_TELEGRAM_ID;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export function initBot(token: string): Telegraf {
  bot = new Telegraf(token);

  bot.start(async (ctx) => {
    const telegramId = String(ctx.from.id);
    const firstName = ctx.from.first_name ?? "Гражданин";

    const [citizen] = await db
      .select()
      .from(citizensTable)
      .where(eq(citizensTable.telegramId, telegramId))
      .limit(1);

    if (citizen) {
      await ctx.reply(
        `🏛 Добро пожаловать в TimeNation, ${citizen.name}!\n\n` +
        `💰 Ваш баланс: *${citizen.balanceMinutes} минут*\n` +
        `📜 Ранг: *${citizen.rank}*\n\n` +
        `Откройте приложение TimeNation, чтобы начать TiMETR-сессию.`,
        { parse_mode: "Markdown" }
      );
    } else {
      await ctx.reply(
        `🏛 Добро пожаловать в TimeNation, ${firstName}!\n\n` +
        `Вы ещё не привязали Telegram к своему аккаунту.\n\n` +
        `Чтобы привязать аккаунт, отправьте команду:\n` +
        `*/link <ваш_номер_телефона>*\n\n` +
        `Например: \`/link +79991234567\``,
        { parse_mode: "Markdown" }
      );
    }
  });

  bot.command("link", async (ctx) => {
    const telegramId = String(ctx.from.id);
    const args = ctx.message.text.split(" ").slice(1);
    const phone = args[0]?.trim();

    if (!phone) {
      await ctx.reply(
        "Укажите номер телефона: */link +79991234567*",
        { parse_mode: "Markdown" }
      );
      return;
    }

    const [citizen] = await db
      .select()
      .from(citizensTable)
      .where(eq(citizensTable.phone, phone))
      .limit(1);

    if (!citizen) {
      await ctx.reply("❌ Гражданин с таким номером не найден. Сначала зарегистрируйтесь в приложении TimeNation.");
      return;
    }

    await db
      .update(citizensTable)
      .set({ telegramId })
      .where(eq(citizensTable.phone, phone));

    await ctx.reply(
      `✅ Аккаунт привязан!\n\n` +
      `👤 Гражданин: *${citizen.name}*\n` +
      `💰 Баланс: *${citizen.balanceMinutes} минут*\n` +
      `📜 Ранг: *${citizen.rank}*\n\n` +
      `Теперь вы будете получать уведомления о каждой транзакции в Казначействе TimeNation.`,
      { parse_mode: "Markdown" }
    );
  });

  bot.command("balance", async (ctx) => {
    const telegramId = String(ctx.from.id);

    const [citizen] = await db
      .select()
      .from(citizensTable)
      .where(eq(citizensTable.telegramId, telegramId))
      .limit(1);

    if (!citizen) {
      await ctx.reply("❌ Аккаунт не привязан. Используйте /link <телефон>");
      return;
    }

    await ctx.reply(
      `💰 *${citizen.name}*\n\n` +
      `Баланс: *${citizen.balanceMinutes} мин*\n` +
      `📜 Ранг: *${citizen.rank}* (Merit: ${citizen.meritScore.toFixed(2)})\n` +
      `Передано: ${citizen.totalGiven} мин\n` +
      `Получено: ${citizen.totalReceived} мин`,
      { parse_mode: "Markdown" }
    );
  });

  // ─── /migratedata — только для администратора ───────────────────────────
  bot.command("migratedata", async (ctx) => {
    const senderId = String(ctx.from.id);

    // Полностью игнорировать для всех не-администраторов
    if (!isAdmin(senderId)) return;

    if (LEGACY_TRANSACTIONS.length === 0) {
      await ctx.reply(
        "⚠️ Массив LEGACY\\_TRANSACTIONS пуст\\.\n\nДобавь исторические записи в telegramService\\.ts перед запуском импорта\\.",
        { parse_mode: "MarkdownV2" }
      );
      return;
    }

    await ctx.reply(
      `⏳ Начинаю импорт *${LEGACY_TRANSACTIONS.length}* исторических транзакций\\.\\.\\.`,
      { parse_mode: "MarkdownV2" }
    );

    let imported = 0;
    let skipped = 0;
    let notFound = 0;
    let errors = 0;

    for (const tx of LEGACY_TRANSACTIONS) {
      try {
        // Декодируем amount: minutes * 100 + seconds
        const minutesTransferred = Math.floor(tx.amount / 100);
        const durationSeconds    = tx.amount % 100;

        // Ищем граждан по имени (без учёта регистра)
        const [fromCitizen] = await db
          .select({ id: citizensTable.id, name: citizensTable.name })
          .from(citizensTable)
          .where(ilike(citizensTable.name, tx.fromName))
          .limit(1);

        const [toCitizen] = await db
          .select({ id: citizensTable.id, name: citizensTable.name })
          .from(citizensTable)
          .where(ilike(citizensTable.name, tx.toName))
          .limit(1);

        if (!fromCitizen || !toCitizen) {
          notFound++;
          logger.warn(
            { fromName: tx.fromName, toName: tx.toName, fromFound: !!fromCitizen, toFound: !!toCitizen },
            "migratedata: citizen not found by name, skipping"
          );
          continue;
        }

        // Дедупликация по (sessionId=0, fromCitizenId, toCitizenId, minutesTransferred)
        const [existing] = await db
          .select({ id: transactionsTable.id })
          .from(transactionsTable)
          .where(
            and(
              eq(transactionsTable.sessionId, 0),
              eq(transactionsTable.fromCitizenId, fromCitizen.id),
              eq(transactionsTable.toCitizenId, toCitizen.id),
              eq(transactionsTable.minutesTransferred, minutesTransferred),
            )
          )
          .limit(1);

        if (existing) {
          skipped++;
          continue;
        }

        await db.insert(transactionsTable).values({
          sessionId: 0,
          fromCitizenId: fromCitizen.id,
          toCitizenId: toCitizen.id,
          fromCitizenName: fromCitizen.name,
          toCitizenName: toCitizen.name,
          minutesTransferred,
          durationSeconds,
          createdAt: new Date(tx.createdAt),
        });

        imported++;
      } catch (err) {
        logger.error({ err, tx }, "migratedata: failed to insert legacy transaction");
        errors++;
      }
    }

    const report =
      `✅ *Импорт завершён*\n\n` +
      `📥 Успешно импортировано: *${imported}* транзакций\n` +
      `⏭ Пропущено (дубли): *${skipped}*\n` +
      `👤 Гражданин не найден: *${notFound}*\n` +
      `❌ Ошибок при записи: *${errors}*`;

    logger.info({ imported, skipped, notFound, errors }, "migratedata: import completed");
    await ctx.reply(report, { parse_mode: "Markdown" });
  });
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  bot.launch().catch((err) => {
    logger.error({ err }, "Telegram bot launch failed");
  });

  process.once("SIGINT", () => bot?.stop("SIGINT"));
  process.once("SIGTERM", () => bot?.stop("SIGTERM"));

  logger.info("Telegram bot started");
  return bot;
}

export async function sendTransactionNotification(
  citizenId: number,
  message: string
): Promise<void> {
  if (!bot) return;

  try {
    const [citizen] = await db
      .select({ telegramId: citizensTable.telegramId })
      .from(citizensTable)
      .where(eq(citizensTable.id, citizenId))
      .limit(1);

    if (!citizen?.telegramId) return;

    await bot.telegram.sendMessage(citizen.telegramId, message, {
      parse_mode: "Markdown",
    });
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const code = (err as { code?: number })?.code;
    if (code === 403) {
      logger.warn({ citizenId }, "Citizen blocked the Telegram bot");
    } else {
      logger.error({ err, citizenId }, "Failed to send Telegram notification");
    }
  }
}

  "Созидатель": "bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/30",
  "Посол": "bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-300 border-blue-500/30",
  "Хранитель Времени": "bg-violet-500/20 text-violet-300 border-violet-500/30",

};

export function Citizens() {
