  useEffect(() => {
    document.title = "Реестр граждан | TimeNation";
  }, []);
  const { data: citizens, isLoading } = useListCitizens({
    query: { queryKey: getListCitizensQueryKey() },
  });

  const filtered = (citizens ?? []).filter(
    (c) =>
      !query ||
      c.name.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase()) ||
      c.phone.includes(query)
  );

  const sorted = [...filtered].sort((a, b) => (b.meritScore ?? 0) - (a.meritScore ?? 0));

  return (
    <Layout>
      <div className="space-y-6">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">Реестр граждан</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground font-mono text-sm mt-1">
            Рейтинг по Merit Score · {citizens?.length ?? "..."} граждан
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="relative max-w-sm">
          <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" />
          <Input
            value={query}
            onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Поиск по имени или номеру..."
            className="pl-9 bg-secondary border-border font-mono text-sm"
          />
        </div>

        <Card className="bg-card border-border">
          <CardHeader className="pb-3">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-mono text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-widest">
              <span className="w-8">#</span>
              <span className="flex-1">Гражданин</span>
              <span className="w-24 text-right">Ранг</span>
              <span className="w-20 text-right">Merit</span>
              <span className="w-24 text-right">Баланс</span>
              <span className="w-20 text-right">Отдал</span>
              <span className="w-20 text-right">Принял</span>
            </div>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="p-0">
            {isLoading ? (
              <div className="p-4 space-y-2">
                {Array.from({ length: 8 }).map((_, i) => (
                  <Skeleton key={i} className="h-10 bg-secondary" />
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : sorted.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm py-8 text-center font-mono">
                Граждан не найдено
              </p>
            ) : (
              <div className="divide-y divide-border/50">
                {sorted.map((citizen, idx) => (
                  <div
                    key={citizen.id}
                    className="flex items-center px-6 py-3 hover:bg-secondary/30 transition-colors"
                  >
                    <span className="w-8 text-xs font-mono text-muted-foreground">{idx + 1}</span>
                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <div className="font-medium text-sm truncate">{citizen.name}</div>
                      <div className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground font-mono">{citizen.phone}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="w-24 text-right">
                      <Badge
                        variant="outline"
                        className={`text-[10px] font-mono truncate max-w-full ${RANK_STYLES[citizen.rank] ?? ""}`}
                      >
                        {citizen.rank}
                      </Badge>
                    </div>
                    <div className="w-20 text-right font-mono text-sm text-primary">
                      {citizen.meritScore ?? 0}
                    </div>
                    <div className="w-24 text-right font-mono text-sm">
                      {(citizen.balanceMinutes as number).toFixed(2)} Д
                    </div>
                    <div className="w-20 text-right font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      {(citizen.totalGiven as number).toFixed(2)} Д
                    </div>
                    <div className="w-20 text-right font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      {(citizen.totalReceived as number).toFixed(2)} Д
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>
    </Layout>
  );
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/web/src/pages/Home.tsx ---
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { Layout } from "@/components/Layout";
import { useGetStats } from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Activity, Clock, Users, Zap } from "lucide-react";

export function Home() {
  const { data: stats, isLoading } = useGetStats();

  useEffect(() => {
    document.title = "Хронос-Консоль | TimeNation";
  }, []);

  return (
    <Layout>
      <div className="space-y-6">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">Хронос-Консоль</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground font-mono text-sm mt-2">Пульс экономики в реальном времени</p>
        </div>

        {isLoading || !stats ? (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
            {[1,2,3,4].map(i => <Skeleton key={i} className="h-32 bg-secondary" />)}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
            <StatCard title="Всего граждан" value={stats.totalCitizens} icon={Users} />
            <StatCard title="Активных граждан" value={stats.activeCitizens} icon={Activity} />
            <StatCard title="Циркулирует (Д)" value={stats.totalMinutesCirculated} icon={Clock} />
            <StatCard title="Транзакций" value={stats.totalTransactions} icon={Zap} />
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6 mt-8">
          <Card className="bg-card border-border">
            <CardHeader>
              <CardTitle className="text-lg font-medium flex items-center gap-2">
                <Clock className="w-5 h-5 text-primary" />
                Последние транзакции
              </CardTitle>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent>
              {isLoading || !stats ? (
                <div className="space-y-4">
                  {[1,2,3,4,5].map(i => <Skeleton key={i} className="h-12 bg-secondary" />)}
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="space-y-4">
                  {stats.recentTransactions.map(tx => (
                    <div key={tx.id} className="flex justify-between items-center p-3 bg-secondary/50 border border-border/50 rounded-sm">
                      <div>
                        <div className="text-sm font-medium">{tx.fromCitizenName} → {tx.toCitizenName}</div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono mt-1">{new Date(tx.createdAt).toLocaleString()}</div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-primary font-mono font-bold">+{(tx.minutesTransferred as number).toFixed(2)} Д</div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                  {stats.recentTransactions.length === 0 && (
                    <div className="text-muted-foreground text-sm py-4 text-center">Транзакций пока нет</div>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
          
          <div className="space-y-6">
             <Card className="bg-card border-border">
              <CardHeader>
                <CardTitle className="text-lg font-medium">Топ — Отдают</CardTitle>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent>
                {isLoading || !stats ? <Skeleton className="h-32 bg-secondary" /> : (
                  <div className="space-y-3">
                    {stats.topGivers.slice(0, 3).map((citizen, i) => (
                      <div key={citizen.id} className="flex justify-between items-center">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                          <span className="text-muted-foreground font-mono text-xs w-4">{i + 1}.</span>
                          <span className="font-medium">{citizen.name}</span>
                        </div>
                        <span className="text-sm font-mono text-muted-foreground">{(citizen.totalGiven as number).toFixed(2)} Д</span>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </CardContent>
            </Card>

            <Card className="bg-card border-border">
              <CardHeader>
                <CardTitle className="text-lg font-medium">Топ — Принимают</CardTitle>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent>
                {isLoading || !stats ? <Skeleton className="h-32 bg-secondary" /> : (
                  <div className="space-y-3">
                    {stats.topReceivers.slice(0, 3).map((citizen, i) => (
                      <div key={citizen.id} className="flex justify-between items-center">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                          <span className="text-muted-foreground font-mono text-xs w-4">{i + 1}.</span>
                          <span className="font-medium">{citizen.name}</span>
                        </div>
                        <span className="text-sm font-mono text-muted-foreground">{(citizen.totalReceived as number).toFixed(2)} Д</span>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Layout>
  );
}

function StatCard({ title, value, icon: Icon }: { title: string, value: string | number, icon: any }) {
  return (
    <Card className="bg-card border-border">
      <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between space-y-0 pb-2">
        <CardTitle className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">{title}</CardTitle>
        <Icon className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent>
        <div className="text-2xl font-bold font-mono text-primary">{value}</div>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/web/src/pages/LaborDashboard.tsx ---
import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";
import { Layout } from "@/components/Layout";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Tabs, TabsContent, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from "@/components/ui/tabs";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import {
  Shield, Clock, AlertTriangle, CheckCircle, ChevronDown, ChevronUp,
  Brain, Users, Settings, Loader2, Lock,
} from "lucide-react";

const API_BASE = "/api";

function govFetch(path: string, password: string, opts: RequestInit = {}) {
  return fetch(`${API_BASE}${path}`, {
    ...opts,
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
      "X-Gov-Password": password,
      ...(opts.headers as Record<string, string> | undefined),
    },
  });
}

// ── Types ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

interface AuditLog {
  id: number;
  citizenId: number | null;
  taskDescription: string;
  estimatedMinutes: number;
  actualMinutes: number | null;
  deviationPercent: number | null;
  complexityLevel: string;
  justification: string;
  requiresCalibration: number;
  moderatedByName: string | null;
  moderatedAt: string | null;
  createdAt: string;
}

interface GovCitizen {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  citizenNumber: string | null;
  govRole: string;
  meritScore: number;
  rank: string;
}

// ── Sub-components ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function ComplexityBadge({ level }: { level: string }) {
  const map: Record<string, string> = {
    low: "bg-green-900/40 text-green-300 border-green-700",
    medium: "bg-yellow-900/40 text-yellow-300 border-yellow-700",
    high: "bg-red-900/40 text-red-300 border-red-700",
  };
  const labels: Record<string, string> = { low: "Низкая", medium: "Средняя", high: "Высокая" };
  return (
    <span className={`inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs border font-medium ${map[level] ?? "bg-secondary text-muted-foreground"}`}>
      {labels[level] ?? level}
    </span>
  );
}

function RoleBadge({ role }: { role: string }) {
  const map: Record<string, string> = {
    citizen: "bg-secondary text-muted-foreground border-border",
    labor_officer: "bg-blue-900/40 text-blue-300 border-blue-700",
    gov_admin: "bg-purple-900/40 text-purple-300 border-purple-700",
  };
  const labels: Record<string, string> = {
    citizen: "Гражданин",
    labor_officer: "Сотрудник Минтруда",
    gov_admin: "Администратор",
  };
  return (
    <span className={`inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs border font-medium ${map[role] ?? "bg-secondary"}`}>
      {labels[role] ?? role}
    </span>
  );
}

// ── Password gate ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function PasswordGate({ onUnlock }: { onUnlock: (pw: string) => void }) {
  const [pw, setPw] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const { toast } = useToast();

  async function handleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/government/auth/verify`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ password: pw }),
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        toast({ title: "Доступ разрешён", description: "Добро пожаловать в кабинет Министерства Труда" });
        onUnlock(pw);
      } else {
        setError("Неверный пароль правительственного контура");
      }
    } catch {
      setError("Ошибка соединения с сервером");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-center min-h-[70vh]">
      <Card className="w-full max-w-md border-yellow-800/40 bg-card">
        <CardHeader className="text-center space-y-3">
          <div className="flex justify-center">
            <div className="p-3 rounded-full bg-yellow-900/30 border border-yellow-700/40">
              <Lock className="w-8 h-8 text-yellow-400" />
            </div>
          </div>
          <CardTitle className="text-xl">Правительственный контур</CardTitle>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            Министерство Труда TimeNation · Только для уполномоченных сотрудников
          </p>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <form onSubmit={(e) => void handleSubmit(e)} className="space-y-4">
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <label className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">Правительственный пароль</label>
              <Input
                type="password"
                placeholder="Введите пароль доступа"
                value={pw}
                onChange={e => setPw(e.target.value)}
                className="bg-secondary border-border"
              />
            </div>
            {error && (
              <p className="text-sm text-red-400 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 flex-shrink-0" /> {error}
              </p>
            )}
            <Button type="submit" className="w-full bg-yellow-600 hover:bg-yellow-500 text-black font-semibold" disabled={loading || !pw}>
              {loading ? <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" /> : <Shield className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" />}
              Войти
            </Button>
          </form>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

// ── Task Auditor tab ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function AuditorTab({ password }: { password: string }) {
  const [taskDesc, setTaskDesc] = useState("");
  const [citizenId, setCitizenId] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<AuditLog | null>(null);
  const { toast } = useToast();

  async function handleAudit() {
    if (!taskDesc.trim()) return;
    setLoading(true);
    setResult(null);
    try {
      const res = await govFetch("/government/audit/task", password, {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          taskDescription: taskDesc,
          citizenId: citizenId ? parseInt(citizenId) : undefined,
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json() as { log?: AuditLog; error?: string };
      if (!res.ok) {
        toast({ title: "Ошибка", description: data.error ?? "Неизвестная ошибка", variant: "destructive" });
        return;
      }
      setResult(data.log ?? null);
      toast({ title: "Аудит выполнен", description: `Оценка: ${data.log?.estimatedMinutes ?? "?"} Д` });
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Ошибка сети", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <Card className="border-border">
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2 text-base">
            <Brain className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-400" />
            Новый аудит задачи
          </CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-4">
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">Описание задачи</label>
            <Textarea
              placeholder="Опишите задачу подробно: технология, объём работы, уровень сложности..."
              value={taskDesc}
              onChange={e => setTaskDesc(e.target.value)}
              className="bg-secondary border-border min-h-[120px] resize-none"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">ID гражданина (необязательно)</label>
            <Input
              type="number"
              placeholder="Например: 42"
              value={citizenId}
              onChange={e => setCitizenId(e.target.value)}
              className="bg-secondary border-border max-w-xs"
            />
          </div>
          <Button
            onClick={() => void handleAudit()}
            disabled={loading || !taskDesc.trim()}
            className="bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-600"
          >
            {loading ? <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" /> : <Clock className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" />}
            Оценить задачу ИИ-аудитором
          </Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {result && (
        <Card className="border-green-800/40 bg-green-950/20">
          <CardHeader>
            <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2 text-base text-green-300">
              <CheckCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
              Результат аудита #{result.id}
            </CardTitle>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="space-y-4">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Оценка времени</p>
                <p className="text-3xl font-bold text-green-300">{result.estimatedMinutes} Д</p>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Сложность</p>
                <div className="mt-1"><ComplexityBadge level={result.complexityLevel} /></div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1">
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Обоснование ИИ</p>
              <p className="text-sm text-foreground/90 leading-relaxed">{result.justification}</p>
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ── Audit Logs tab ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function LogsTab({ password }: { password: string }) {
  const [logs, setLogs] = useState<AuditLog[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [calibOnly, setCalibOnly] = useState(false);
  const [expandedId, setExpandedId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [actualInputs, setActualInputs] = useState<Record<number, string>>({});
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const { toast } = useToast();

  const loadLogs = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await govFetch(
        `/government/audit/logs${calibOnly ? "?calibrationOnly=true" : ""}`,
        password
      );
      const data = await res.json() as { logs?: AuditLog[] };
      setLogs(data.logs ?? []);
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Ошибка загрузки логов", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [password, calibOnly, toast]);

  useEffect(() => { void loadLogs(); }, [loadLogs]);

  async function submitActual(log: AuditLog) {
    const val = parseFloat(actualInputs[log.id] ?? "");
    if (!val || val <= 0) { toast({ title: "Введите корректное время", variant: "destructive" }); return; }
    setSubmitting(log.id);
    try {
      const res = await govFetch(`/government/audit/logs/${log.id}`, password, {
        method: "PATCH",
        body: JSON.stringify({ actualMinutes: val }),
      });
      const data = await res.json() as { log?: AuditLog; error?: string };
      if (!res.ok) { toast({ title: "Ошибка", description: data.error, variant: "destructive" }); return; }
      toast({
        title: `Отклонение: ${data.log?.deviationPercent?.toFixed(1) ?? "?"}%`,
        description: data.log?.requiresCalibration ? "⚠️ Требует калибровки" : "✓ В норме",
      });
      void loadLogs();
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Ошибка сети", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(null);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm cursor-pointer">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={calibOnly}
              onChange={e => setCalibOnly(e.target.checked)}
              className="rounded"
            />
            Только требующие калибровки
          </label>
        </div>
        <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => void loadLogs()} disabled={loading}>
          {loading ? <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" /> : "Обновить"}
        </Button>
      </div>

      {loading ? (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-12">
          <Loader2 className="w-6 h-6 animate-spin text-muted-foreground" />
        </div>
      ) : logs.length === 0 ? (
        <Card className="border-dashed">
          <CardContent className="py-12 text-center text-muted-foreground text-sm">
            Записей не найдено
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {logs.map(log => (
            <Card key={log.id} className={`border transition-colors ${log.requiresCalibration ? "border-orange-700/40 bg-orange-950/10" : "border-border"}`}>
              <div
                className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 cursor-pointer"
                onClick={() => setExpandedId(expandedId === log.id ? null : log.id)}
              >
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                    {log.requiresCalibration === 1 && (
                      <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-orange-400 flex-shrink-0" />
                    )}
                    <span className="text-sm font-medium truncate">{log.taskDescription}</span>
                    <ComplexityBadge level={log.complexityLevel} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    <span>#{log.id}</span>
                    <span>Оценка: <span className="text-foreground font-mono">{log.estimatedMinutes} Д</span></span>
                    {log.actualMinutes != null && (
                      <span>Факт: <span className="font-mono">{log.actualMinutes} Д</span></span>
                    )}
                    {log.deviationPercent != null && (
                      <span className={log.requiresCalibration ? "text-orange-400" : "text-green-400"}>
                        Δ {log.deviationPercent.toFixed(1)}%
                      </span>
                    )}
                    <span>{new Date(log.createdAt).toLocaleString("ru")}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
                {expandedId === log.id ? <ChevronUp className="w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" /> : <ChevronDown className="w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" />}
              </div>

              {expandedId === log.id && (
                <div className="px-4 pb-4 border-t border-border pt-4 space-y-4">
                  <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground leading-relaxed">{log.justification}</p>

                  {log.actualMinutes == null && (
                    <div className="flex items-end gap-2">
                      <div className="space-y-1 flex-1 max-w-xs">
                        <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Фактическое время (мин / Д)</label>
                        <Input
                          type="number"
                          placeholder="0"
                          value={actualInputs[log.id] ?? ""}
                          onChange={e => setActualInputs(prev => ({ ...prev, [log.id]: e.target.value }))}
                          className="bg-secondary border-border h-8 text-sm"
                        />
                      </div>
                      <Button
                        size="sm"
                        onClick={() => void submitActual(log)}
                        disabled={submitting === log.id}
                        className="bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-600 h-8"
                      >
                        {submitting === log.id ? <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" /> : "Записать"}
                      </Button>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {log.moderatedAt && (
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      Промодерировано: {new Date(log.moderatedAt).toLocaleString("ru")}
                      {log.moderatedByName ? ` · ${log.moderatedByName}` : ""}
                    </p>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ── System Prompt tab ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function PromptTab({ password }: { password: string }) {
  const [promptText, setPromptText] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const { toast } = useToast();

  useEffect(() => {
    void (async () => {
      try {
        const res = await govFetch("/government/system-prompt", password);
        const data = await res.json() as { promptText?: string };
        setPromptText(data.promptText ?? "");
      } catch { /* ignore */ } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    })();
  }, [password]);

  async function handleSave() {
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const res = await govFetch("/government/system-prompt", password, {
        method: "PUT",
        body: JSON.stringify({ promptText }),
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        toast({ title: "Промт обновлён", description: "Новые настройки ИИ-аудитора применены" });
      } else {
        const d = await res.json() as { error?: string };
        toast({ title: "Ошибка", description: d.error, variant: "destructive" });
      }
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Ошибка сети", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <Card className="border-border">
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2 text-base">
            <Settings className="w-4 h-4 text-purple-400" />
            Системный промт ИИ-аудитора
          </CardTitle>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            Инструкции, которые получает Gemini при каждом запросе на аудит. Изменения вступают в силу немедленно.
          </p>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-4">
          {loading ? (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" /> Загрузка…
            </div>
          ) : (
            <>
              <Textarea
                value={promptText}
                onChange={e => setPromptText(e.target.value)}
                className="bg-secondary border-border min-h-[400px] resize-none font-mono text-sm"
              />
              <Button
                onClick={() => void handleSave()}
                disabled={saving || promptText.length < 20}
                className="bg-purple-700 hover:bg-purple-600"
              >
                {saving ? <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" /> : <Settings className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" />}
                Сохранить промт
              </Button>
            </>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

// ── Citizens tab ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function CitizensTab({ password }: { password: string }) {
  const [citizens, setCitizens] = useState<GovCitizen[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [updating, setUpdating] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const { toast } = useToast();

  useEffect(() => {
    void (async () => {
      try {
        const res = await govFetch("/government/citizens", password);
        const data = await res.json() as { citizens?: GovCitizen[] };
        setCitizens(data.citizens ?? []);
      } catch { /* ignore */ } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    })();
  }, [password]);

  async function setRole(citizenId: number, govRole: string) {
    setUpdating(citizenId);
    try {
      const res = await govFetch(`/government/citizens/${citizenId}/gov-role`, password, {
        method: "PATCH",
        body: JSON.stringify({ govRole }),
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        setCitizens(prev => prev.map(c => c.id === citizenId ? { ...c, govRole } : c));
        toast({ title: "Роль обновлена" });
      }
    } catch { /* ignore */ } finally {
      setUpdating(null);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {loading ? (
        <div className="flex justify-center py-12">
          <Loader2 className="w-6 h-6 animate-spin text-muted-foreground" />
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-1">
          {citizens.map(c => (
            <div key={c.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-lg bg-secondary/50 hover:bg-secondary transition-colors">
              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="text-sm font-medium">{c.name}</span>
                  {c.citizenNumber && <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono">#{c.citizenNumber}</span>}
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-0.5">
                  <RoleBadge role={c.govRole} />
                  <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{c.rank} · {c.meritScore.toFixed(0)} Merit</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <select
                value={c.govRole}
                disabled={updating === c.id}
                onChange={e => void setRole(c.id, e.target.value)}
                className="text-xs bg-background border border-border rounded px-2 py-1 text-foreground"
              >
                <option value="citizen">Гражданин</option>
                <option value="labor_officer">Сотрудник Минтруда</option>
                <option value="gov_admin">Администратор</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export function LaborDashboard() {
  const [password, setPassword] = useState<string | null>(
    () => sessionStorage.getItem("gov_pw")
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    document.title = "Министерство Труда | TimeNation";
  }, []);

  function handleUnlock(pw: string) {
    sessionStorage.setItem("gov_pw", pw);
    setPassword(pw);
  }

  if (!password) {
    return (
      <Layout>
        <PasswordGate onUnlock={handleUnlock} />
      </Layout>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Layout>
      <div className="space-y-6">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-yellow-900/30 border border-yellow-700/40">
            <Shield className="w-5 h-5 text-yellow-400" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight">Министерство Труда</h1>
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground font-mono">Правительственный контур TimeNation · ИИ-Аудитор Времени</p>
          </div>
          <div className="ml-auto">
            <Button
              variant="outline"
              size="sm"
              onClick={() => { sessionStorage.removeItem("gov_pw"); setPassword(null); }}
            >
              <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 mr-1.5" /> Выйти

            <LogsTab password={password} />
          </TabsContent>
          <TabsContent value="prompt" className="mt-4">
            <PromptTab password={password} />
          </TabsContent>

            <CitizensTab password={password} />

// --- FILE: artifacts/web/src/pages/PassportLookup.tsx ---
import { useState } from "react";
import { Layout } from "@/components/Layout";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Search, FileText, CreditCard, Clock, AlertCircle, ExternalLink, Shield } from "lucide-react";

interface LookupResult {
  status: "issued" | "pending";
  citizenNumber: string;
  name?: string;
  rank?: string;
  idCardUrl?: string;
  passportUrl?: string;
  message?: string;
}

export function PassportLookup() {
  const [input, setInput] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<LookupResult | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function handleLookup(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    const raw = input.trim().replace(/\D/g, "");
    if (!raw) return;

    setLoading(true);
    setResult(null);
    setError(null);

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/citizens/lookup/${raw}`);
      const data = await res.json() as LookupResult & { error?: string };
      if (!res.ok) {
        setError(data.error ?? "Гражданин не найден в реестре");
        return;
      }
      setResult(data);
    } catch {
      setError("Ошибка соединения с реестром. Проверьте подключение.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <Layout>
      <div className="max-w-lg space-y-6">

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <Shield className="w-7 h-7 text-primary shrink-0" />
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-mono font-bold text-foreground tracking-widest uppercase">
              Реестр граждан
            </h1>
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground font-mono mt-0.5">
              Восстановление доступа к документам
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <Card className="bg-card border-border">
          <CardContent className="p-6">
            <form onSubmit={handleLookup} className="space-y-4">
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <label className="text-xs font-mono text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-widest">
                  Номер гражданина (9 цифр)
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={input}
                    onChange={e => {
                      const v = e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, "").slice(0, 9);
                      setInput(v);
                    }}
                    placeholder="000000000"
                    maxLength={9}
                    className="w-full bg-background border border-border rounded-sm px-4 py-3 font-mono text-xl tracking-[0.3em] text-center text-foreground placeholder:text-muted-foreground/40 focus:outline-none focus:border-primary transition-colors"
                  />
                </div>
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono text-center">
                  Номер выдан при регистрации. Только цифры.
                </p>
              </div>

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading || input.replace(/\D/g, "").length === 0}
                className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-primary hover:bg-primary/90 disabled:opacity-40 text-primary-foreground font-mono font-bold py-3 px-4 rounded-sm transition-colors"
              >
                {loading ? (
                  <span className="animate-pulse">Поиск в реестре…</span>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Search className="w-4 h-4" />
                    ПОЛУЧИТЬ ДОКУМЕНТЫ
                  </>
                )}
              </button>
            </form>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        {error && (
          <Card className="bg-destructive/10 border-destructive/30">
            <CardContent className="p-5 flex items-start gap-3">
              <AlertCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-destructive shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
              <div>
                <p className="font-mono font-semibold text-destructive text-sm">Гражданин не найден</p>
                <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs font-mono mt-1">{error}</p>
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        )}

        {result?.status === "pending" && (
          <Card className="bg-yellow-500/10 border-yellow-500/30">
            <CardContent className="p-5 flex items-start gap-3">
              <Clock className="w-5 h-5 text-yellow-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
              <div>
                <p className="font-mono font-semibold text-yellow-400 text-sm uppercase tracking-wide">
                  Верификация в процессе
                </p>
                <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs font-mono mt-1">
                  {result.message}
                </p>
                <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs font-mono mt-2">
                  Номер гражданина:{" "}
                  <span className="text-foreground font-bold tracking-widest">{result.citizenNumber}</span>
                </p>
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        )}

        {result?.status === "issued" && (
          <Card className="bg-card border-border">
            <CardContent className="p-6 space-y-5">
              <div className="pb-4 border-b border-border space-y-1">
                <p className="text-xs font-mono text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-widest">
                  Гражданин подтверждён
                </p>
                <p className="text-2xl font-mono font-bold text-primary tracking-widest">
                  {result.citizenNumber}
                </p>
                <p className="text-base font-semibold text-foreground">{result.name}</p>
                {result.rank && (
                  <p className="text-xs font-mono text-muted-foreground">{result.rank}</p>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-3">
                <p className="text-xs font-mono text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-widest">
                  Документы
                </p>
                <a
                  href={result.idCardUrl}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  className="flex items-center gap-3 w-full bg-primary/10 hover:bg-primary/20 border border-primary/40 text-primary font-mono font-bold py-4 px-5 rounded-sm transition-colors"
                >
                  <CreditCard className="w-5 h-5 shrink-0" />
                  <span className="flex-1 uppercase tracking-wide text-sm">ID-карта TimeNation</span>
                  <ExternalLink className="w-4 h-4 opacity-60" />
                </a>
                <a
                  href={result.passportUrl}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  className="flex items-center gap-3 w-full bg-muted/40 hover:bg-muted/70 border border-border text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground font-mono py-4 px-5 rounded-sm transition-colors"
                >
                  <FileText className="w-5 h-5 shrink-0" />
                  <span className="flex-1 uppercase tracking-wide text-sm">Паспорт (классический)</span>
                  <ExternalLink className="w-4 h-4 opacity-60" />
                </a>
              </div>

              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono text-center pt-1">
                Откройте ссылку и сохраните файл через «Сохранить как» в браузере
              </p>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        )}

      </div>
    </Layout>
  );
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/web/src/pages/Register.tsx ---
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { Layout } from "@/components/Layout";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { UserPlus, Clock, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle, Shield, Award, ExternalLink, Upload, Camera, Lock, BookOpen, PenLine } from "lucide-react";
import { SignaturePad } from "@/components/SignaturePad";

const BASE_WEB = import.meta.env.BASE_URL?.replace(/\/$/, "") ?? "";
const MANIFESTO_URL = "https://issuu.com/timenation2025/docs/_timenatio";

function pad2(n: number) { return String(n).padStart(2, "0"); }
function formatCountdown(s: number) {
  return `${pad2(Math.floor(s / 3600))}:${pad2(Math.floor((s % 3600) / 60))}:${pad2(s % 60)}`;
}

type Mode = "form" | "pending" | "issued";
interface PendingData { citizenNumber: string; availableAt: string; citizenId: number; citizenName: string; }

export function Register() {
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [surname, setSurname] = useState("");
  const [givenNames, setGivenNames] = useState("");
  const [dateOfBirth, setDateOfBirth] = useState("");
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [photo, setPhoto] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [photoPreview, setPhotoPreview] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [signatureBase64, setSignatureBase64] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [manifestoAccepted, setManifestoAccepted] = useState(false);
  const [tosAccepted, setTosAccepted] = useState(false);
  const [privacyAccepted, setPrivacyAccepted] = useState(false);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode>("form");
  const [pendingData, setPendingData] = useState<PendingData | null>(null);
  const [countdown, setCountdown] = useState(3600);
  const pollRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => { document.title = "Стать гражданином | TimeNation"; }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (mode !== "pending" || !pendingData) return;
    const computeLeft = () => Math.max(0, Math.ceil((new Date(pendingData.availableAt).getTime() - Date.now()) / 1000));
    setCountdown(computeLeft());
    const tick = setInterval(() => setCountdown(computeLeft()), 1000);
    const poll = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/citizens/number/${pendingData.citizenNumber}/status`);
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const data = await res.json() as { status: string };
        if (data.status === "issued") {
          clearInterval(tick);
          if (pollRef.current) clearInterval(pollRef.current);
          setMode("issued");
        }
      } catch { /* silent */ }
    };
    pollRef.current = setInterval(poll, 15_000);
    return () => { clearInterval(tick); if (pollRef.current) clearInterval(pollRef.current); };
  }, [mode, pendingData]);

  function handlePhotoChange(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0] ?? null;
    setPhoto(file);
    setPhotoPreview(file ? URL.createObjectURL(file) : null);
  }

  const canSubmit = tosAccepted && privacyAccepted && manifestoAccepted && !!signatureBase64;

  async function handleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!canSubmit) return;
    setError(null);
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const n = name.trim() || `${surname.trim()} ${givenNames.trim()}`.trim();
      const formData = new FormData();
      formData.append("name", n);
      formData.append("phone", phone.trim());
      if (surname.trim()) formData.append("surname", surname.trim());
      if (givenNames.trim()) formData.append("givenNames", givenNames.trim());
      if (dateOfBirth.match(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/)) formData.append("dateOfBirth", dateOfBirth);
      if (email.trim()) formData.append("email", email.trim());
      formData.append("privacyAgreementAccepted", "true");
      if (photo) formData.append("passport_photo", photo);
      if (signatureBase64) formData.append("signatureBase64", signatureBase64);

      const res = await fetch("/api/citizens", { method: "POST", body: formData });
      const data = await res.json() as { id?: number; name?: string; citizenNumber?: string; availableAt?: string; error?: string; };
      if (!res.ok) { setError(data.error ?? "Ошибка регистрации"); return; }
      if (data.citizenNumber && data.availableAt) {
        setPendingData({ citizenNumber: data.citizenNumber, availableAt: data.availableAt, citizenId: data.id!, citizenName: data.name! });
        setMode("pending");
      }
    } catch {
      setError("Ошибка соединения с сервером");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  /* ── Pending screen ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  if (mode === "pending" && pendingData) {
    return (
      <Layout>
        <div className="max-w-lg space-y-5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 p-5 bg-primary/10 border border-primary/30 rounded-sm">
            <Shield className="w-8 h-8 text-primary shrink-0" />
            <div>
              <h2 className="font-bold text-xl">Верификация данных</h2>
              <p className="text-muted-foreground font-mono text-sm mt-1">Ваши данные приняты. Инспекция займёт ровно 60 минут.</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <Card className="bg-card border-border text-center">
            <CardContent className="p-8 space-y-2">
              <p className="text-xs font-mono text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-widest">ОСТАЛОСЬ</p>
              <p className={`text-6xl font-mono font-bold tabular-nums tracking-widest ${countdown <= 0 ? "text-emerald-400" : "text-foreground"}`}>
                {formatCountdown(countdown)}
              </p>
              {countdown <= 0 && <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground font-mono animate-pulse">Получаем паспорт…</p>}
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
          <Card className="bg-card border-border">
            <CardContent className="p-6 space-y-3">
              <p className="text-xs font-mono text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">НОМЕР ГРАЖДАНИНА</p>
              <p className="text-3xl font-mono font-bold text-primary tracking-widest">{pendingData.citizenNumber}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono">Сохраните этот номер — он ваш идентификатор в TimeNation</p>
              <div className="pt-3 border-t border-border">
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono leading-relaxed">
                  Закрыли вкладку? Документы всегда доступны по номеру на странице{" "}
                  <a href={`${BASE_WEB}/passport-lookup`} className="text-primary underline underline-offset-2 hover:text-primary/80 transition-colors">
                    Реестр граждан
                  </a>
                </p>
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </div>
      </Layout>
    );
  }

  /* ── Issued screen ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  if (mode === "issued" && pendingData) {
    return (
      <Layout>
        <div className="max-w-lg space-y-5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 p-5 bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/30 rounded-sm">
            <Award className="w-8 h-8 text-emerald-400 shrink-0" />
            <div>
              <h2 className="font-bold text-xl">Паспорт выдан!</h2>
              <p className="text-muted-foreground font-mono text-sm mt-1">Верификация завершена. Добро пожаловать в TimeNation.</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <Card className="bg-card border-border">
            <CardContent className="p-6 space-y-4">
              <div>
                <p className="text-xs font-mono text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">НОМЕР ГРАЖДАНИНА</p>
                <p className="text-3xl font-mono font-bold text-primary tracking-widest">{pendingData.citizenNumber}</p>
                <p className="text-base font-medium mt-2">{pendingData.citizenName}</p>
              </div>
              <a
                href={`/api/citizens/number/${pendingData.citizenNumber}/id-card`}
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer"
                className="flex items-center gap-2 w-full justify-center bg-emerald-500 hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white font-mono font-bold py-3 px-4 rounded-sm transition-colors"
              >
                <ExternalLink className="w-4 h-4" />
                Открыть ID-карту
              </a>
              <a
                href={`/api/citizens/number/${pendingData.citizenNumber}/passport`}
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer"
                className="flex items-center gap-2 w-full justify-center bg-muted/60 hover:bg-muted border border-border text-muted-foreground font-mono text-sm py-2.5 px-4 rounded-sm transition-colors"
              >
                <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                Паспорт (классический формат)
              </a>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </div>
      </Layout>
    );
  }

  /* ── Registration form ──────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  return (
    <Layout>
      <div className="max-w-lg space-y-6">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">Стать гражданином</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground font-mono text-sm mt-1">Каждый гражданин получает 1 Дармин — родовую минуту</p>
        </div>

        {/* ── Manifesto read banner ──────────────────────────────────────── */}
        <div className="p-4 bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/30 rounded-sm space-y-3">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
            <BookOpen className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
            <div>
              <p className="font-semibold text-sm">Перед подачей заявки ознакомьтесь с документом</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono mt-0.5">
                Регистрация возможна только после прочтения основополагающего документа TimeNation.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <a
            href={MANIFESTO_URL}
            target="_blank"
            rel="noopener noreferrer"
            className="flex items-center gap-2 w-full justify-center bg-amber-500/20 hover:bg-amber-500/30 border border-amber-500/40 text-amber-300 font-mono font-semibold text-sm py-2.5 px-4 rounded-sm transition-colors"
          >
            <ExternalLink className="w-4 h-4" />
            Открыть документ TimeNation (Issuu)
          </a>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-start gap-3 p-4 bg-primary/5 border border-primary/20 rounded-sm">
          <Clock className="w-5 h-5 text-primary mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
          <div className="space-y-1 text-sm">
            <p className="font-medium">1 Дармин = 1 родовая минута</p>
            <p className="text-muted-foreground font-mono text-xs">Родовая минута неотчуждаема · Верификация 60 мин · Паспорт выдаётся автоматически</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <Card className="bg-card border-border">
            <CardHeader>
              <CardTitle className="text-base font-medium">Данные для паспорта ICAO Doc 9303</CardTitle>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent className="space-y-4">

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div className="space-y-1.5">
                  <Label className="text-xs font-mono text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wide">Фамилия</Label>
                  <Input value={surname} onChange={e => setSurname(e.target.value)} placeholder="Иванов" className="bg-secondary border-border font-mono" />
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-1.5">
                  <Label className="text-xs font-mono text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wide">Имя и отчество</Label>
                  <Input value={givenNames} onChange={e => setGivenNames(e.target.value)} placeholder="Иван Иванович" className="bg-secondary border-border font-mono" />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <Label className="text-xs font-mono text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wide">Псевдоним / отображаемое имя</Label>
                <Input value={name} onChange={e => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="Если нет ФИО — укажите псевдоним" className="bg-secondary border-border font-mono" />
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <Label className="text-xs font-mono text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wide">Дата рождения</Label>
                <Input value={dateOfBirth} onChange={e => setDateOfBirth(e.target.value)} placeholder="ГГГГ-ММ-ДД" className="bg-secondary border-border font-mono" />
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <Label className="text-xs font-mono text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wide">Номер телефона *</Label>
                <Input type="tel" value={phone} onChange={e => setPhone(e.target.value)} placeholder="+7XXXXXXXXXX" required className="bg-secondary border-border font-mono" />
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <Label className="text-xs font-mono text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wide">E-mail для получения паспорта</Label>
                <Input type="email" value={email} onChange={e => setEmail(e.target.value)} placeholder="example@timenation.com" className="bg-secondary border-border font-mono" />
              </div>

              {/* ── Photo upload ─────────────────────────────────────────── */}
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <Label className="text-xs font-mono text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wide flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Camera className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  Фотография гражданина (для паспорта)
                </Label>
                <label className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center w-full border-2 border-dashed border-border rounded-sm cursor-pointer bg-secondary hover:bg-secondary/80 transition-colors overflow-hidden">
                  {photoPreview ? (
                    <div className="relative w-full">
                      <img src={photoPreview} alt="Предпросмотр" className="w-full h-48 object-cover" />
                      <div className="absolute bottom-2 right-2 bg-black/60 text-white text-xs font-mono px-2 py-1 rounded">Нажмите для замены</div>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-8 gap-2">
                      <Upload className="w-8 h-8 text-muted-foreground" />
                      <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground font-mono"><span className="font-semibold">Загрузить фото анфас</span></p>
                      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono">Светлый фон · JPG, PNG · до 5 МБ</p>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <input id="passport_photo" name="passport_photo" type="file" className="hidden" accept="image/*" onChange={handlePhotoChange} />
                </label>
              </div>

              {/* ── Signature pad ────────────────────────────────────────── */}
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <Label className="text-xs font-mono text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wide flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <PenLine className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  Подпись гражданина *
                </Label>
                <SignaturePad onChange={setSignatureBase64} />
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono">
                  {signatureBase64 ? "✓ Подпись поставлена" : "Распишитесь мышью или пальцем на планшете"}
                </p>
              </div>

              {/* ── Three compliance checkboxes ───────────────────────────── */}
              <div className="space-y-3 pt-2 border-t border-border/50">

                {/* 1. Document read confirmation */}
                <div className="flex items-start gap-3 p-3 bg-amber-500/5 border border-amber-500/25 rounded-sm">
                  <BookOpen className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                  <div className="flex-1 flex items-start gap-2">
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      id="manifesto"
                      checked={manifestoAccepted}
                      onChange={e => setManifestoAccepted(e.target.checked)}
                      className="mt-0.5 accent-primary"
                      required
                    />
                    <label htmlFor="manifesto" className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono leading-relaxed">
                      Я ознакомлен с{" "}
                      <a href={MANIFESTO_URL} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="text-amber-400 underline">
                        основополагающим документом TimeNation
                      </a>.
                      {" "}Я понимаю принципы и правила цифрового государства.
                    </label>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* 2. TOS */}
                <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                  <input type="checkbox" id="tos" checked={tosAccepted} onChange={e => setTosAccepted(e.target.checked)} className="mt-0.5 accent-primary" />
                  <label htmlFor="tos" className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono leading-relaxed">
                    Я ознакомлен с{" "}
                    <a href={`${BASE_WEB}/tos`} target="_blank" className="text-primary underline">Условиями использования</a>.
                    {" "}Дармины — внутриигровые баллы, не являются денежными средствами.
                  </label>
                </div>

                {/* 3. Privacy / GDPR */}
                <div className="flex items-start gap-3 p-3 bg-muted/20 border border-border/40 rounded-sm">
                  <Lock className="w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                  <div className="flex-1 flex items-start gap-2">
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      id="privacy"
                      checked={privacyAccepted}
                      onChange={e => setPrivacyAccepted(e.target.checked)}
                      className="mt-0.5 accent-primary"
                      required
                    />
                    <label htmlFor="privacy" className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono leading-relaxed">
                      Я даю согласие на обработку моих персональных данных (ФИО, email, фото) в целях выпуска цифрового удостоверения TimeNation.
                      Данные хранятся в защищённой базе и не передаются третьим лицам.
                      Вправе отозвать в любой момент (GDPR / 152-ФЗ).
                    </label>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {error && (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 bg-destructive/10 border border-destructive/30 rounded-sm text-sm text-destructive font-mono">
                  <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
                  {error}
                </div>
              )}

              <Button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading || !canSubmit}
                className="w-full bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary/90 font-mono font-bold disabled:opacity-40"
              >
                {loading ? "Регистрация…" : (
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <UserPlus className="w-4 h-4" />
                    Подать заявку на гражданство
                  </span>
                )}
              </Button>

              {!canSubmit && (
                <p className="text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono">
                  {!signatureBase64 ? "Поставьте подпись, затем отметьте все три пункта" : "Отметьте все три пункта, чтобы продолжить"}
                </p>
              )}

              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 bg-muted/30 rounded-sm">
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-primary shrink-0" />
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono">
                  Паспорт выдаётся через 60 минут. Доступ к системе — немедленно.
                </p>
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </form>
      </div>
    </Layout>
  );
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/web/src/pages/Shop.tsx ---
import { Layout } from "@/components/Layout";
import {
  useGetMinutePackages,
  getGetMinutePackagesQueryKey,
  useCreateCheckoutSession,
} from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { ShoppingBag, Clock, AlertCircle } from "lucide-react";
import { useCitizen } from "@/context/CitizenContext";
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";

export function Shop() {
  const { citizen } = useCitizen();

  useEffect(() => {
    document.title = "Казначейство | TimeNation";
  }, []);
  const [loadingPkg, setLoadingPkg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const { data: packages, isLoading } = useGetMinutePackages({
    query: { queryKey: getGetMinutePackagesQueryKey() },
  });

  const checkout = useCreateCheckoutSession({
    mutation: {
      onSuccess(data) {
        if (data.url) {
          window.location.href = data.url;
        } else {
          setError("Не удалось получить ссылку на оплату");
        }
        setLoadingPkg(null);
      },
      onError(err: unknown) {
        const msg = (err as { message?: string })?.message ?? "Ошибка";
        setError(msg);
        setLoadingPkg(null);
      },
    },
  });

  function handleBuy(packageId: string) {
    if (!citizen) {
      setError("Войдите в аккаунт в боковом меню");
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    setLoadingPkg(packageId);
    const base = window.location.origin + "/web/shop";
    checkout.mutate({
      data: {
        citizenId: citizen.id,
        packageId,
        successUrl: base + "?status=success",
        cancelUrl: base + "?status=cancel",
      },
    });
  }

  const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
  const status = urlParams.get("status");

  return (
    <Layout>
      <div className="space-y-6 max-w-2xl">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">Казначейство</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground font-mono text-sm mt-1">
            Пополнение баланса Дарминов через Stripe
          </p>
        </div>

        {status === "success" && (
          <div className="p-4 bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/30 rounded-sm text-emerald-300 text-sm font-mono">
            Оплата прошла успешно. Дармины будут зачислены в течение нескольких секунд.
          </div>
        )}
        {status === "cancel" && (
          <div className="p-4 bg-secondary border border-border rounded-sm text-muted-foreground text-sm font-mono">
            Оплата отменена. Вы можете попробовать снова.
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Citizen info or login prompt */}
        {citizen ? (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 bg-primary/10 border border-primary/30 rounded-sm">
            <div className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-primary shrink-0" />
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <span className="text-sm font-mono text-primary font-bold">{citizen.name}</span>
              <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono ml-3">Баланс: {(citizen.balanceMinutes as number).toFixed(2)} Д</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 bg-yellow-400/10 border border-yellow-400/30 rounded-sm text-yellow-300 text-sm font-mono">
            <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
            Войдите в аккаунт в боковом меню, чтобы купить Дармины
          </div>
        )}

        {error && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 bg-destructive/10 border border-destructive/30 rounded-sm text-sm text-destructive font-mono">
            <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Packages */}
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {isLoading ? (
            Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, i) => (
              <Skeleton key={i} className="h-24 bg-secondary" />
            ))
          ) : (packages ?? []).map((pkg) => (
            <Card
              key={pkg.id}
              className="bg-card border-border hover:border-primary/50 transition-colors"
            >
              <CardContent className="flex items-center justify-between p-5">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                  <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-sm bg-primary/10 border border-primary/30 flex items-center justify-center">
                    <Clock className="w-5 h-5 text-primary" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <div className="font-bold text-lg font-mono text-primary">
                      {pkg.minutes} Д
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono mt-0.5">
                      {pkg.label}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                  <div className="text-right">
                    <div className="font-bold font-mono text-lg">
                      {pkg.price} {pkg.currency}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono">
                      {(pkg.price / pkg.minutes).toFixed(2)} {pkg.currency}/Д
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <Button
                    onClick={() => handleBuy(pkg.id)}
                    disabled={loadingPkg !== null || !citizen}
                    className="bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary/90 font-mono"
                  >
                    {loadingPkg === pkg.id ? (
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="animate-spin text-xs">⟳</span> Переход...
                      </span>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <ShoppingBag className="w-4 h-4" />
                        Купить
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>

        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono leading-relaxed">
          Дармины — внутренняя игровая валюта TimeNation. Не являются денежными средствами
          в юридическом смысле. Оплата обрабатывается через Stripe.
        </p>
      </div>
    </Layout>
  );
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/web/src/pages/Timeline.tsx ---
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { Layout } from "@/components/Layout";
import {
  useGetTimelineOccupied,
  getGetTimelineOccupiedQueryKey,
} from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, MapPin } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

const MONTHS = [
  "Январь", "Февраль", "Март", "Апрель", "Май", "Июнь",
  "Июль", "Август", "Сентябрь", "Октябрь", "Ноябрь", "Декабрь",
];

const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();
const currentMonth = new Date().getMonth() + 1;

export function Timeline() {
  const [year, setYear] = useState(currentYear);
  const [month, setMonth] = useState(currentMonth);

  useEffect(() => {
    document.title = "Таймлайн | TimeNation";
  }, []);

  const yearStr = String(year);
  const monthStr = String(month);

  const { data: occupied, isLoading } = useGetTimelineOccupied(
    { year: yearStr, month: monthStr },
    { query: { queryKey: getGetTimelineOccupiedQueryKey({ year: yearStr, month: monthStr }) } }
  );

  const coords = occupied ?? [];

  // Group by day within the month
  const byDay: Record<string, string[]> = {};
  for (const entry of coords) {
    const coord = entry.timelineCoord;
    const day = coord.slice(6, 8);
    if (!byDay[day]) byDay[day] = [];
    byDay[day].push(coord);
  }

  const daysInMonth = new Date(year, month, 0).getDate();

  function changeMonth(delta: number) {
    let m = month + delta;
    let y = year;
    if (m > 12) { m = 1; y++; }
    if (m < 1) { m = 12; y--; }
    setMonth(m);
    setYear(y);
  }

  function formatCoord(coord: string): string {
    // YYYYMMDDHHmm → YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm
    return `${coord.slice(0, 4)}-${coord.slice(4, 6)}-${coord.slice(6, 8)} ${coord.slice(8, 10)}:${coord.slice(10, 12)}`;
  }

  return (
    <Layout>
      <div className="space-y-6">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">Таймлайн</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground font-mono text-sm mt-1">
            Захваченные временны́е координаты · 12-значный код
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Month navigator */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
          <Button
            variant="outline"
            size="sm"
            onClick={() => setYear((y) => y - 1)}
            className="font-mono text-xs"
          >
            {year - 1}
          </Button>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" onClick={() => changeMonth(-1)}>
              <ChevronLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
            </Button>
            <span className="font-mono font-bold text-lg w-40 text-center">
              {MONTHS[month - 1]} {year}
            </span>
            <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" onClick={() => changeMonth(1)}>
              <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
            </Button>
          </div>
          <Button
            variant="outline"
            size="sm"
            onClick={() => setYear((y) => y + 1)}
            className="font-mono text-xs"
          >
            {year + 1}
          </Button>
          <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono ml-2">
            {coords.length} точек
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* Calendar grid */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-7 gap-1 text-xs font-mono">
          {["Пн","Вт","Ср","Чт","Пт","Сб","Вс"].map((d) => (
            <div key={d} className="text-center text-muted-foreground uppercase py-1">{d}</div>
          ))}
          {/* Offset for first day of month */}
          {(() => {
            const firstDay = new Date(year, month - 1, 1).getDay();
            const offset = firstDay === 0 ? 6 : firstDay - 1;
            return Array.from({ length: offset }).map((_, i) => (
              <div key={`off-${i}`} />
            ));
          })()}
          {Array.from({ length: daysInMonth }).map((_, i) => {
            const day = String(i + 1).padStart(2, "0");
            const entries = byDay[day] ?? [];
            const hasCoords = entries.length > 0;
            return (
              <div
                key={day}
                className={`aspect-square flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-sm border transition-colors ${
                  hasCoords
                    ? "bg-primary/15 border-primary/50 text-primary"
                    : "bg-secondary/30 border-border/30 text-muted-foreground"
                }`}
                title={hasCoords ? entries.map(formatCoord).join("\n") : ""}
              >
                <span className="text-[11px] font-bold">{i + 1}</span>
                {hasCoords && (
                  <span className="text-[8px] font-mono text-primary/80">{entries.length}</span>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Coordinate list */}
        {isLoading ? (
          <div className="space-y-2">
            {Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => (
              <Skeleton key={i} className="h-10 bg-secondary" />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : coords.length > 0 && (
          <Card className="bg-card border-border">
            <CardHeader>
              <CardTitle className="text-sm font-medium flex items-center gap-2">
                <MapPin className="w-4 h-4 text-primary" />
                Захваченные координаты — {MONTHS[month - 1]} {year}
              </CardTitle>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-2">
                {coords.map((entry) => (
                  <div
                    key={entry.timelineCoord}
                    className="p-2 bg-secondary/50 border border-border/50 rounded-sm text-xs font-mono"
                  >
                    <div className="text-primary font-bold">{entry.timelineCoord}</div>
                    <div className="text-muted-foreground text-[10px] mt-0.5">
                      {formatCoord(entry.timelineCoord)}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        )}

        {!isLoading && coords.length === 0 && (
          <div className="text-center py-12 text-muted-foreground font-mono text-sm">
            В этом месяце нет захваченных координат
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </Layout>
  );
}

// --- FILE: artifacts/web/src/pages/Tv.tsx ---
import { useState, useRef, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";
import { Layout } from "@/components/Layout";
import {
  useGetTvSlots,
  useGetTvSchedule,
  getGetTvSlotsQueryKey,
  getGetTvScheduleQueryKey,
} from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import {
  ExternalLink,
  Video,
  Clock,
  Terminal,
  Zap,
  Shield,
  CreditCard,
  Wallet,
  ChevronRight,
  Bitcoin,
  Radio,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useCitizen } from "@/context/CitizenContext";

// ── Types ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

interface SlotLike {
  id: number;
  slotType: string;
  status: string;
  minuteIndex: number;
  hourLabel: string;
  minLabel: string;
  ownerName?: string | null;
  isRetro: boolean;
  timeltrBurnCost: number;
}

interface BookingResult {
  checkoutUrl?: string;
  slot?: { id: number; status: string };
  message?: string;
}

// ── Constants ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const SLOT_TYPE_COLORS: Record<string, string> = {
  citizen: "bg-primary/30 border-primary/50",
  quarter: "bg-blue-500/30 border-blue-400/50",
  half: "bg-purple-500/30 border-purple-400/50",
  apex: "bg-rose-500/30 border-rose-400/50",
};

const TYPE_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  citizen: "Гражданин",
  quarter: "Квартал",
  half: "Половина",
  apex: "Апекс",
};

const SLOT_TYPE_BADGE: Record<string, string> = {
  citizen: "bg-primary/20 text-primary border-primary/40",
  quarter: "bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-300 border-blue-400/40",
  half: "bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-300 border-purple-400/40",
  apex: "bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border-rose-400/40",
};

const RENDER_PATH_BY_SLOT: Record<string, "light" | "heavy"> = {
  citizen: "light",
  quarter: "heavy",
  half: "heavy",
  apex: "heavy",
};

// ── Chronos Live Player ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

interface LiveSlotInfo {
  minuteIndex: number;
  videoUrl: string | null;
  hasContent: boolean;
  ownerName: string | null;
  slotType: string | null;
  slotId: number | null;
}

function ChronosLivePlayer({ date }: { date: string }) {
  const videoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement>(null);

  const [currentMinute, setCurrentMinute] = useState<number>(() => {
    const now = new Date();
    return now.getUTCHours() * 60 + now.getUTCMinutes();
  });
  const [utcClock, setUtcClock] = useState("");
  const [slot, setSlot] = useState<LiveSlotInfo | null>(null);
  const prefetch = useRef<{ minute: number; data: LiveSlotInfo | null } | null>(null);

  const fetchSlot = useCallback(async (minute: number): Promise<LiveSlotInfo | null> => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/tv/live-slot?date=${date}&minute=${minute}`);
      if (!res.ok) return null;
      return (await res.json()) as LiveSlotInfo;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }, [date]);

  // Switch the player to a new slot info
  const applySlot = useCallback((info: LiveSlotInfo | null) => {
    setSlot(info);
    const v = videoRef.current;
    if (!v) return;
    if (info?.videoUrl) {
      if (v.src !== info.videoUrl) {
        v.src = info.videoUrl;
        v.load();
      }
      void v.play().catch(() => {/* autoplay blocked — user must click */});
    } else {
      v.pause();
      v.removeAttribute("src");
      v.load();
    }
  }, []);

  // Initial load
  useEffect(() => {
    const now = new Date();
    const m = now.getUTCHours() * 60 + now.getUTCMinutes();
    setCurrentMinute(m);
    void fetchSlot(m).then(applySlot);
  }, [fetchSlot, applySlot]);

  // 500ms ticker: clock + minute-change engine + prefetch
